| OI-14 | Process for submitting a separate support report when a video is considered inappropriate; distinguish from in-run reject/regenerate | Product + Privacy + Safety | Before support operations | No channel for an operator to receive media; by default the operator must not view content | Outside the V1 flow; decision and access rights not fixed |
| OI-15 | AI evaluation set with real child drawings with approved consent/authorization, access, purpose, transfer and retention | AI + Privacy/Legal | Before claims about real-world quality | A synthetic benchmark does not represent quality on real drawings | Not part of the demo; not yet created |
| OI-16 | Included quota values of the Parent account/school organization, runs/month limit and storage capacity | Product + Billing + AI + Operations | After benchmark; before commercial launch | Cannot release quota price/allowance or forecast storage | Configuration left open; numbers not fixed |
| OI-17 | Wireframe/interaction sign-off for the profile form, lesson extraction, Teacher Sketch Deck, script approval, quota/top-up, projection, delete/cancel and error/loading/accessibility states | Product + Design + Accessibility reviewer | Before UI implementation | Technical screens may not suit the quick operation of Parent/Teacher or may omit states | Wireframes not yet designed; the text flows at §5/§19/§21 are the baseline |
| OI-18 | Resumable upload adapter POC on MinIO/local storage: write each application chunk as a separate object, receipt/hash/expiry in `core.upload_sessions` and `core.upload_parts`, idempotent replay, bounded assemble, final single PutObject, abort/TTL cleanup and AV boundary. Do not use the S3 Multipart Upload API; verify the behavior of the pinned MinIO SDK. | Backend + Mobile + Infra | Before Parent/Teacher media upload integration | If the SDK/storage semantics or the POC do not satisfy checksum, resume and cleanup, media upload is not enabled | Target design fixed; POC still open |
| OI-19 | Synthetic POC for the SVG catalog/engine and structured-vector draft: activity coverage, 4–8 progressive steps, recognizability, age fit, safety, hash/lineage, license, latency, render performance and compute cost | AI + Teacher/Parent Product/Design | Before enabling AI SVG guide/visual | A guide/illustration that is hard to understand, missing steps, unsafe or slow may affect teaching; no child data is sent to compensate for quality | FLUX is no longer a target, except for the optional Teacher Sketch Deck picture (§31.21: step-count fidelity and license still OPEN); SVG source/catalog, Qwen draft policy, license, quality floor, caps and benchmark protocol remain OPEN |
| OI-20 | Lifecycle classification of each BE1/classroom/demo route currently mounted versus the single-child Parent/Teacher target: keep as a compatibility surface, replace with a versioned target contract, or deprecate with a plan | Product + Architecture + Backend | Before API contract freeze/external pilot | The presence of a route may be misread as an accepted product or bring legacy collaboration behavior into the new UI/flow; each operation must be mapped from API Catalog Rev37 | Open; the target SRS still prohibits classroom collaboration/multi-child story runs; no deprecation/migration allocation yet |

Sections §29.2 are the deployment conditions corresponding to OI-05–OI-16; this table is the single consolidated source for owner, milestone, impact, and status. OI-18 is the technical POC for the upload session/part tables and the object-PUT chunk contract; OI-19 is the quality/cost gate for Teacher Sketch. Details of the model/provider/payment payload will be finalized according to official documentation/sandbox in the ADR/implementation contract; the provider's signatures or payloads must not be simulated in the SRS.

<a id="srs-15"></a>
## 15. Sources and Level of Reliability

### Repository sources reviewed

- Repository operating rules in AGENTS.md and docs/governance.
- docs/architecture/OVERVIEW.md, PYTHON_BACKEND_ARCHITECTURE.md, DEPENDENCY_RULES.md, CONTRACTS_AND_INTEGRATION.md.
- Proposed ADR-0001, ADR-0017, ADR-0018, ADR-0019, ADR-0020, ADR-0021, and ADR-0022.
- Context, decisions, source register, approval, and plan of FEAT-063; the current SQLAlchemy/Core table declarations; the list of Alembic revisions, the manifest, and compose.yaml.
- Rev45 source review: `backend/src/sketch2life/main.py`, `interfaces/http/app.py`, `interfaces/http/router_composition.py`, `interfaces/http/routers/images.py`, `interfaces/http/middleware/bounded_image_upload.py`, `apps/mobile/README.md` and package manifests, `apps/ui-mobile/BACKEND_INTEGRATION.md`, the Teacher UI package manifest, `backend/Dockerfile`, and `compose.yaml`. This is source evidence and does not replace the runtime/test results of Rev43.
- The API contract/catalog Rev31–Rev37 and EV-063-33/EV-063-34 are used to distinguish route declarations, mounted routes, OpenAPI snapshots, and verified functionality.
- The architecture guides in the repository are supplementary boundary/implementation documents and do not replace the SRS/ADR for product direction. Rev45 found Lightning/Runpod provider wording in some older guides; the current cross-check must follow the canonical SRS and authoritative ADRs, and the Lightning adapter still in the source must be regarded as implementation history/demo and not be taken as a Modal/production decision.
- The earlier draft of the FEAT-062 flow/architecture; the PixiJS motion assumption in the old documents has been superseded.
- The FEAT-050 video runtime decision sheet and the FEAT-003 multimodal evidence. NO_GO applies only to the previously evaluated Qwen3-VL profile; it must not be extended to Qwen3.5-VL. Qwen3.5-VL is the development direction chosen by the owner but requires a separate POC.

### Official technical/legal sources cross-checked on 2026-10-27
- [Amazon S3 multipart upload limits](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/qfacts.html): a multipart part is at least 5 MiB (except the last part); this application chunk design uses separate object PUTs and does not use the S3 Multipart Upload API.
- [MinIO Go Client API: PutObject](https://docs.min.io/aistor/developers/sdk/go/api/): the current documentation describes objects under 128 MiB as being sent with a single PUT; the project's exact SDK version has not been verified and still requires a POC before implementation.

- [LTX-2.5 system requirements](https://docs.ltx.io/open-source-model/getting-started/system-requirements) — states a minimum GPU VRAM of 32 GB and Python/CUDA requirements.
- [Modal GPU guide](https://modal.com/docs/guide/gpu) — lists L4/L40S, L40S with 48 GB GPU RAM; prices may change.
- [Modal local data](https://modal.com/docs/guide/local-data), [data residency](https://modal.com/docs/guide/data-residency), [Volumes](https://modal.com/docs/guide/volumes), [cloud bucket mounts](https://modal.com/docs/guide/cloud-bucket-mounts) — limit how the demo can stage files from local MinIO and explain why real children's media is not yet sent.
- [Supported Python versions](https://devguide.python.org/versions/) — Python 3.13 is the proposed target runtime; verify runtime/library compatibility before upgrading the repository.
- [PostgreSQL releases](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/release.html) and [versioning policy](https://www.postgresql.org/support/versioning/) — PostgreSQL 17 is the repo/demo baseline; PostgreSQL 18 must be evaluated before migration/deployment.
- [Firebase verify ID tokens](https://firebase.google.com/docs/auth/admin/verify-id-tokens) — the backend verifies the client ID token and obtains the UID.
- [Firebase Authentication email action links](https://firebase.google.com/docs/auth/admin/email-action-links) — the Firebase Admin SDK generates email verification/password reset links to be sent through an email adapter; the action code belongs to Firebase Authentication, so no internal token table is needed.
- [Modal function invocation methods](https://modal.com/docs/guide/function-invocation-methods), [Modal FunctionCall SDK](https://modal.com/docs/sdk/py/latest/FunctionCall) — a long-running call may be submitted asynchronously, its call ref stored, and then polled; an RQ worker must not be held waiting for the GPU.
- [PostgreSQL 17 Row Security](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/ddl-rowsecurity.html) — RLS policy and warnings about table owner/BYPASSRLS; app runtime roles must not own tables.
- [Android Play Integrity](https://developer.android.com/google/play/integrity) — Android app/device integrity signal verified by the backend; it does not replace user authentication.
- [Web Push API](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Push_API), [UnifiedPush distributors](https://unifiedpush.org/users/distributors/), [UnifiedPush Android specification](https://unifiedpush.org/developers/spec/android/) — browser/Android push options. Android provider remains a synthetic POC gate; Firebase messaging is excluded by repository's Firebase-Authentication-only rule.
- [ClamAV scanning usage](https://docs.clamav.net/manual/Usage) — candidate scanner/quarantine workflow; the verdict is fail-closed before the parser/model.
- [RQ worker types](https://python-rq.org/docs/workers/) — worker scheduling/fairness reference; verify RQ/Redis license and deployment fit before adoption.
- [Temporal workflow execution](https://docs.temporal.io/workflow-execution), [Temporal tasks and timers](https://docs.temporal.io/tasks) — reference for durable workflow state, human-wait timers and recovery; Temporal is an alternative to evaluate, not a selected dependency.
- [Redis license terms](https://redis.io/legal/licenses/) — license review is a deployment gate for the proposed Redis version.
- [PostgreSQL 17 Indexes](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/indexes.html) — reference for indexes/partial indexes for state-dependent uniqueness.
- [MoMo Subscription](https://developers.momo.vn/v3/docs/payment/api/wallet/subscription/) — recurring subscription flow, frequency, request ID, and payment schedule.
- [VNPAY Recurring Payment technical specification](https://sandbox.vnpayment.vn/apis/files/VNPAYGW_RecurringPayment_Techspec2.1.0-VN.pdf) — recurring flow for merchants. Merchant approval and the specific contract remain an integration gate.
- [Qwen3.5-9B model card](https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B) — reference for the Qwen3.5-VL development direction; the exact checkpoint/runtime must still be pinned and verified. [VieNeu-TTS](https://huggingface.co/pnnbao-ump/VieNeu-TTS) remains a historical candidate reference, not a selected TTS checkpoint.
- [Law No. 91/2025/QH15 on Personal Data Protection](https://chinhphu.vn/?classid=1&docid=214590&orggroupid=1&pageid=27160) — Government document record; the SRS does not conclude legal compliance; legal/privacy experts must review before processing real children's data or transferring data to an AI provider.
- [Consolidated Law on Children No. 108/VBHN-VPQH](https://vanban.chinhphu.vn/?classid=2629&docid=215227&pageid=27160) — the official consolidated text serving as child-safety context; the project needs professional legal review and must not infer compliance from architecture documents.
- [W3C WCAG 2.2](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) — accessibility standard for web content; the Level AA target is a design requirement, not a certification.
- [W3C WCAG2ICT](https://www.w3.org/TR/wcag2ict/) — guidance on applying WCAG to non-web software such as Android; informative guidance that does not by itself create a conformance claim.
- [Google Play Families Policy Requirements](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/9893335) — requirements to be reviewed before determining the target audience/releasing the app.
- [ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148:2018](https://www.iso.org/standard/72089.html) and [IEEE 830-1998 status](https://standards.ieee.org/ieee/830/1222/) — 29148 is the current reference for requirements engineering; 830-1998 has been superseded. This is not a compliance claim for this SRS.

### Reliability Limits

- The figure 86 is a declared mapping in the source, not the result of introspecting a running database. The column inventory of the 51 tables in the register is extracted from the repository and still needs to be reconciled against the actual migrations/schema.
- Model candidates, cost, performance, and character consistency have not been benchmarked in this document.
- The new table fields and target actions in the register are a proposed logical design, not DDL that has been run. The current column list is a source declaration and does not prove that the deployed database matches.
- This SRS is a product/architecture baseline for review and does not replace legal advice or evidence of deployed behavior.
- The owner chose to review the documents in Vietnamese, keeping technical names unchanged for consistent use. The SRS and the data-table register must be approved before starting the diagram/PDF.

## 17. Requirements traceability to architecture

| Requirement group | Owner domain/data | Contract/operation | Acceptance evidence required in the later implementation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Role/account/profile separation | Parent, BE1 | OwnerScopeRefV1, ProfileRefV1 | Authorization contract fixtures and evidence of blocking cross-scope access |
| One child/one active run | Story, AI Jobs | StoryRunCommandV1, AiJobCommandV1 | Database constraints and evidence of duplicate/concurrent creation |
| Organization class/roster/capacity | BE1, Billing | Entitlement port, organization command | Capacity/concurrency boundary tests and assignment authorization |
| Lesson and plan import | Lesson, Learning Content | LessonOccurrenceRefV1, AssetRefV1 | Format/size/OCR/revision/approval contract |
| Story/context/review | Profile owner, Story | Profile snapshot and ScriptRevisionV1 | Snapshot allowlist, revision hash, rewrite/approval state |
| Image/audio/lesson upload that resumes after interruption | Core Upload Session + Core Asset | UploadSessionV1, UploadPartReceiptV1, AssetRefV1 | Resume/chunk conflict/hash/TTL/AV POC; the session/part tables are the canonical source of upload state |
| Video creation/playback | Story, AI Jobs, Core Assets | AiJobStatusV1, AssetRefV1 | Retry, stale result, media validation and correct-scope playback |
| Billing and notices | Billing, Notifications | PaymentEventV1, NotificationCommandV1 | Signature/idempotency, grace/capacity and notification dedupe |
| Quota entitlement/usage | Billing (sole write owner) | VideoQuotaAdmissionV1, VideoQuotaSummaryV1; separate AI Policy compute reservation port; Story holds opaque refs | Grant, FIFO, idempotent exhaustion/top-up; no cross-module SQL writes/joins |
| Class projection | Lesson, Story/Core Asset | ClassProjectionConfirmationV1 | Occurrence confirmation, assignment revoke and short-lived token |
| Security/operations | Core, interfaces/infrastructure | Error envelope, telemetry convention, rate-limit policy | Redaction, 429, dashboard, backup/restore, secret scan |
| Legacy retention | Existing owner module | Non-destructive phased Alembic, compatibility and feature flag | Before/after inventory, reconciliation and restore test |

This draft document does not assert that implementation evidence exists.

<a id="srs-18"></a>
## 18. Glossary, assumptions and system constraints

### 18.1 Glossary

| Term | Definition |
|---|---|
| Adult account | An authenticated adult identity within a product role scope. Parent and Teacher are two separate accounts even though one person may hold both roles. |
| Parent profile | A child profile owned by a single Parent account; separate from the profile managed by a Teacher. |
| Managed profile | A child profile belonging to an organization and administered through BE1. At any time the profile belongs to one active class roster. |
| Class | An organization-owned roster container with exactly one active responsible Teacher. No collaboration runtime is created. |
| Teaching occurrence | A teaching session/period of a class, by date or name, with one imported/reviewed lesson and one approved plan. This is the unit of one sample video. |
| Lesson plan | A structure interpreting the source lesson of an occurrence, reviewed by the Teacher; it has learning objectives, a story-telling direction and one or more selected activities. |
| Story run | One end-to-end processing record for one child, from drawing/idea to script/video; the active-owner lock belongs to the Parent account or the current responsible Teacher, not to the creator. |
| Active owner/current owner | The Parent account that owns a Parent run, or the Teacher currently assigned by BE1 to be responsible for the class of a Teacher run. `created_by_actor_ref` is provenance only and grants no permission after transfer. |
| Adult-wait / `ABANDONED` | A state with no running AI job that needs an adult action. Reminder at +144 hours; at +168 hours `VIDEO_READY` becomes `COMPLETED`, and other adult-wait states become `ABANDONED`; content is retained until the user deletes it. |
| Upload session / application chunk | A durable session with actor/scope/purpose, expiry and receipts; a chunk of ≤4 MiB is an independent PutObject object. This is not a part of the S3 Multipart Upload API. |
| Sample video | The output representing one Teacher occurrence. The initial video and quality regenerations remain one sample run for one child. |
| Story revision | A generated, immutable version of the script/storyboard. Each run has the first version and at most three AI rewrites. |
| Quality video regeneration | A full-video request after an adult sees that the output is unsuitable and returns to view the script. At most two times after the first request; an accepted request consumes one quota unit even on a technical failure/safety block. |
| Video quota grant | An immutable entitlement batch issued by Billing to an owner scope. Included grants are issued monthly and expire at the end of the month; top-ups do not expire and carry forward until used. |
| Quota admission | An idempotent decision that accepts one full-video request and allocates one available unit from a valid grant before provider dispatch. Separate from rate limit and GPU concurrency. |
| Class projection | The Teacher controls the display of the single sample video of the occurrence to the whole class after the responsible Teacher confirms the audience/purpose. No class login or reusable playback capability is granted. |
| Technical retry | Automatic retry of scene generation due to an infrastructure/output error. At most two retries after the first attempt for each scene. |
| Approved activity catalog | Outdoor activities that have a version, are approved and published by content administration for Parents to choose from and Teachers to reference. |
| Teacher-specific activity | An activity that applies to only one occurrence, entered by the responsible Teacher and confirmed through a safety checklist. |
| Minimal context snapshot | An immutable selection from the permitted profile/context, approved by an adult for one run; it contains exactly one child. |
| Source asset | The original drawing/audio/lesson uploaded by an adult; always distinguished from model output. |
| Stale result | An asynchronous result whose expected run/revision/input hash does not match the current state; it is recorded but not applied. |
| Synthetic demo data | Simulated names, profile facts, artwork, lesson text and audio for development/demo that do not identify real children. |

### 18.2 Assumptions

- Parent/Teacher onboarding already exists or will be built on the current Firebase Authentication. How a Teacher is invited/activated and how the adult is verified is an implementation decision; the Teacher role must not be inferred from a client claim.
- The organization has at least one admin; each active class has exactly one responsible Teacher. A class cannot operate if an assignment is missing.
- The Teacher imports the source lesson before creating the occurrence plan. The occurrence uses the source revision for review and activity selection.
- Adults have enough time to review the child's voice transcript, the image subject, the activity/context and the script. The product does not release a video if adult interaction is missing at the script confirmation gate.
- A Parent/Teacher may end the run after viewing the ready video. If there is no meaningful action in any adult-wait state (including DRAFT), the system reminds at +144 hours and closes at +168 hours; `VIDEO_READY` becomes COMPLETED, other states become ABANDONED. Content is retained until the user deletes it; the reminder email is sent best-effort only if the current owner has a verified email.
- The local demo uses synthetic content. Real child content is not a fixture or a temporary way of operating.
- The backend worker connects to Modal through an authenticated provider transport. Local MinIO is not accessed directly by Modal.
- The owner separately configures the real subscription price and merchant account. The SRS does not set a VND price itself.

### 18.3 Constraints

- One child/run is an invariant in the database, API, job, storage lineage and UI, not just a flow convention.
- Parent and Teacher identity/profile do not share a primary key, owner scope or authorization path.
- The legacy session ID keeps its earlier collaboration meaning and is not an alias of story_run_id.
- The GPU image/model/library revision, parser library, email vendor, production host/region and storage endpoint need to be pinned in a later phase.
- There is no absolute guarantee of preserving identity in the video; this is a quality goal measured on synthetic drawings and rated by adults.
- Public video URLs, download endpoints or storage credentials must not reside in the client.

<a id="srs-19"></a>
## 19. Detailed use cases and alternative flows

The use cases do not depend on a specific implementation. Route names and storage mapping are proposals in §9 and the table catalog. Every write operation requires a verified principal, current permission, idempotency where applicable, and a server-side state transition.

### UC-01 — Parent logs in and creates a child profile

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | Parent |
| Preconditions | Firebase Authentication succeeds; the backend can resolve an account with the Parent role; the profile scope belongs to this account |
| Trigger | Parent selects Add child |
| Main flow | The backend validates the bounded profile fields → creates the Parent profile head → creates an immutable revision containing age in months/age group and adult-entered context → returns the profile ID and an opaque revision |
| Alternative flows | An invalid age/group returns a field-level error; duplicate display names are allowed; an unsupported role or missing Parent scope is rejected |
| Postcondition | Only the owner can view the profile. No link is created to a Teacher profile, class or organization. |
| Data/requirements | parent.child_profiles, parent.profile_revisions, parent.profile_context_entries; FR-C-01/04/05 |

Intake of a Teacher profile serves the same user purpose but uses the organization-managed profile aggregate, a structured form and the current class roster permissions. The parent form is a source of information provided by an adult; it is not entered by OCR.

### UC-02 — Parent creates and views a story video

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | Parent |
| Preconditions | The Parent has permission on the profile; this adult has no other unfinished run. Topic and lesson are optional; an image is required. One main activity must be selected before the story is created. |
| Trigger | Parent starts creating a story |
| Main flow | The Parent may select a topic or pin an exact lesson/version to view the knowledge presentation; the guide SVG is optional → capture/upload one drawing (mandatory) → enter text and/or record audio → ASR, and the adult confirms the transcript → VLM analyzes the image and cross-checks against the topic/lesson if present → if there is a mismatch, notify the user to change the topic or recapture at most once; after the second image, use the subject recognized by the VLM, and the lesson remains the knowledge source → the adult confirms the subject/context → catalog-first activity suggestions; the adult must choose exactly one primary activity before the script, and a request for another set must exclude options that have already been displayed/rejected → confirm context → AI generates the script/storyboard → at most three rewrites within the limit, and adult review → confirm the final script → video admission and the current pipeline run asynchronously → technical/safety validation and private playback → no-pin displays the primary activity saved in the run; lesson pin displays all activities of the exact pinned version and marks the primary activity. |
| Alternative flow | Reject audio that is too long/too large before inference; editing the transcript creates a new revision; editing the subject re-runs the VLM; the user may request at most three story rewrites; reject model output that is in the wrong format/stale; when technical retries are exhausted, mark the run as failed; if the video is ready but not suitable, return to the script review step within the regeneration limit to improve quality |
| Postcondition | The run is bound to one child, the context snapshot and the primary activity confirmed before the story, the optional pinned lesson version, and immutable script/video provenance; the primary activity is presented after the video, together with all activities of the pinned lesson if any. The private artifact can be played; no download URL is returned. |
| Data/requirements | story.*; ai_jobs.*; core.asset_metadata/core.asset_lineage; FR-P and FR-AI |

### UC-03 — Teacher creates an organization-managed profile

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | Responsible Teacher; the School Manager of the organization may manage roster members |
| Preconditions | The Teacher has an organization assignment and an active class; the class is within its capacity limit |
| Trigger | The Teacher selects Add child |
| Main flow | The Teacher enters structured fields → the backend verifies class responsibility and organization scope → creates the profile head and an immutable revision → creates an active enrollment for the selected class → the profile appears in that class's roster |
| Alternative flow | Profiles with duplicate display names are allowed; if the profile already has an active enrollment in another class, the enrollment must be transferred/closed first; a suspended class is read-only; the capacity limit blocks creating a new class, but does not block profile changes in an existing class |
| Postcondition | A profile belonging to an organization has at most one active class enrollment and is not linked to a Parent account. |
| Data/requirements | be1.managed_profiles, be1.enrollments, be1.profile_revisions/context; FR-T-02/04/05 |

### UC-04 — Teacher imports and approves a lesson plan occurrence

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | Responsible Teacher |
| Preconditions | The Teacher is assigned to the selected class; the occurrence allows writing; the source file is of a supported type and within limits |
| Trigger | The Teacher imports a lesson plan for a teaching occurrence |
| Main flow | Upload the original file → check the extension, content signature, encryption and size → extract text without OCR → store extraction provenance → the Teacher reviews and edits the text if needed → create an immutable edit revision → determine the objective/story direction/activity list → confirm the safety checklist for the individual activity → approve the plan revision |
| Alternative flow | Unsupported format, encrypted file, >25 MB, >100 pages/slides, corrupt archive, or text that cannot be extracted returns a specific safe error code; scanned pages are marked OCR_NOT_SUPPORTED and are not OCR'd; the Teacher may replace the file or edit the extracted text themselves into a new revision |
| Postcondition | One occurrence has an approved plan that references a specific text revision of the document. The plan may list multiple activities; there is no child run yet. |
| Data/requirements | lesson.occurrences, source_documents, document_revisions, plan_revisions, plan_activities; FR-T-06–09 |

### UC-05 — Teacher creates a single-child sample video for an occurrence

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | Responsible Teacher |
| Preconditions | The occurrence plan is approved; a sample video slot remains; the Teacher is still responsible; the roster and eligibility/consent policy are approved; the system creates a run only after selecting a child under that policy and the Teacher confirms the selection/context; the adult has no other active run |
| Trigger | The Teacher starts a sample video request; the system randomly selects one child from the eligible set under the approved policy and presents it to the Teacher for confirmation |
| Main flow | The system builds the eligible set under the eligibility/consent policy approved by the owner and randomly selects one child; the Teacher reviews and confirms the child/context before the run starts → create a run bound to exactly one child/occurrence/approved plan → pin one selected activity → review the minimal profile context → capture via browser or upload a phone photo → add text/voice → review the transcript and subject → AI drafts the script → the Teacher may request rewrites within the limit and then confirms the script → the application reserves the worst-case GPU budget via AI Policy, then Billing grants atomic/idempotent quota admission → run the video job → the video that passes the safety check is shown immediately to the responsible Teacher → before showing it to the class, the Teacher confirms the audience/purpose of use for this occurrence → the Teacher finishes or marks it as unsuitable → if unsuitable, the script must be reviewed/confirmed and quota must be granted before regenerating to improve quality |
| Alternative flow | No eligible child: stop selection, keep the occurrence in its current state, create no sample run/job/quota/claim, no fallback, and show only a generic message that there is no valid choice; do not reveal the per-child exclusion reasons. A stale candidate may be reselected at most once. If the Teacher has not confirmed, the run is not accepted; selection expiry/resume and audit evidence remain OPEN per Q-01. Reject any request with multiple child IDs, a child outside the current roster/assignment, or a stale plan; if quota is exhausted, return VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED and do not dispatch; an accepted request consumes quota even if it later fails technically/is blocked for safety; a technical failure or safety block before playback leaves the occurrence's sample slot free; after one playable sample video has been accepted and passed safety, no other independent sample run may be started for the occurrence; regeneration to improve quality is allowed only within the same run; if the presentation is not confirmed, no class-projection token is issued |
| Postcondition | At most one playable sample video that has passed the safety check is kept for the occurrence. That video is bound to one child and one activity. |
| Data/requirements | lesson.*, story.*, ai_jobs.*, billing.video_quota_grants/video_quota_usage; ai_policy.resource_* only for GPU/compute metering; notifications.inbox_items/push_endpoints; FR-T-10–16, FR-AI, FR-B-10–14, BR-09–17, BR-38 |

### UC-06 — School Manager manages capacity and classes

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | School Manager of the organization |
| Preconditions | The School Manager has permission to manage classes and can read the subscription/capacity; how the permission is stored/resolved remains open at §31.10 |
| Trigger | The School Manager creates a class, buys slots, assigns a Teacher, or reduces capacity |
| Main flow | The backend reads the effective capacity from Billing → the class-creation transaction locks/checks for a free slot → creates the class → the School Manager assigns one responsible Teacher → grant roster permission |
| Alternative flow | When capacity is exhausted, creating a new class returns CLASS_CAPACITY_REACHED; the School Manager may switch to the billing flow to buy more capacity; concurrent class-creation operations are serialized; a suspended class is view-only; when downgrading, the classes to be suspended must be chosen |
| Postcondition | The number of active classes does not exceed the effective capacity; each write-enabled class has one responsible Teacher; media viewing permission is managed separately. |
| Data/requirements | be1.classrooms/assignments; billing.capacity_periods; FR-T-02/03, FR-B |

### UC-07 — Adult deletes a run in COMPLETED, FAILED, or ABANDONED

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The Parent owner, or the Teacher currently assigned as responsible for the class; the former creator retains only provenance |
| Preconditions | The run is in COMPLETED, FAILED, or ABANDONED; the caller has permission; the run is not already being deleted |
| Trigger | The adult selects Delete session/run |
| Main flow | The API verifies the Parent owner or the current responsible Teacher → record a lifecycle request → block issuing new playback tokens → delete the transcript, input audio/drawing, context snapshot, story revision, generated scene/video assets, and eligible private derived assets → record completion and a metadata-only audit |
| Alternative flow | A failure to delete the current object is retried idempotently and the status remains pending; shared assets are kept until other permitted references are reconciled; a former Teacher after reassignment no longer has permission to delete, and the new responsible Teacher is handled under the lifecycle policy |
| Postcondition | The run content is deleted per the lifecycle. Billing invoices and minimal audit metadata are unaffected. There is no separate video deletion operation or trash-recovery waiting period. |
| Data/requirements | be1.data_lifecycle_requests, core.asset_metadata/lineage, story.*; FR-C-08, BR-26 |

### UC-08 — School Manager renews or changes organization capacity

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | School Manager of the organization with billing permission |
| Preconditions | The organization has a billing contact, a selected billing period, and a configured price version |
| Trigger | Purchase/upgrade a plan, change the auto-renewal setting, renewal falls due, payment fails, or downgrade |
| Main flow | Create an invoice snapshot → start the subscription/payment operation via MoMo/VNPAY → verify the provider callback → record a unique event and payment attempt per transaction → update the subscription/capacity → enqueue the notification |
| Alternative flow | Returning from the browser before the callback is verified leaves the invoice pending; a duplicate callback is acknowledged idempotently; a failed payment starts a grace period; recovery within the grace period returns the subscription to active; when the grace period ends, creating new classes/runs is restricted; a downgrade schedules suspension of the selected classes at the effective point |
| Postcondition | The capacity/payment state is auditable and notifications are deduplicated. No provider credentials or card/payment instrument secrets are stored. |
| Data/requirements | billing.*, notifications.*; FR-B-01–09, FR-B-13 |

### UC-09 — Adult requests deletion of a child profile

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The Parent profile owner; for a Teacher profile, the authorized Teacher or the organization lifecycle actor per the final role policy |
| Preconditions | The caller has permission over the profile; no deletion request is already active |
| Trigger | The caller selects Delete child profile |
| Main flow | The backend verifies the owner scope → marks the profile as unavailable for new runs → revokes profile/run/playback access → creates a lifecycle request → schedules deletion of linked runs/context/media → closes the active enrollment if any → reconciles asset references → records completion and a minimal audit |
| Alternative flow | If a job is running, request cancellation and discard late-arriving output; an object deletion failure keeps the lifecycle pending and retries; profiles belonging to another owner scope are not affected; current legal/financial audit requirements continue to be managed separately |
| Postcondition | The profile content and linked run media are deleted per the lifecycle; no hidden link to the remaining profile scope is created; the organization's enrollment history may retain metadata containing no content to explain roster history |
| Data/requirements | parent.child_profiles/data_authorizations; be1.managed_profiles/enrollments/data_lifecycle_requests; Core lifecycle asset; FR-C-10, BR-35 |

### UC-10 — Manage video quota

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | A Parent buys for their own Parent account; an assigned School Manager buys for the organization and manages Teacher allocation; a Teacher only views/uses the limit allocated by the organization; the Platform Admin manages offer/price |
| Prerequisites | The actor is authenticated and has a current scope; a Parent uses only their own Parent account, and a School Manager uses only the organization they are assigned to. A purchase requires an active offer, a configured provider, and a price version determined by the server; auto-renew requires explicit authorization and provider support |
| Trigger | A Platform Admin creates or versions an offer or price; a Parent/School Manager views quota or purchases a plan; the purchaser cancels auto-renew; a School Manager changes a Teacher's allocation within the organization |
| Main flow | A Platform Admin creates, versions, and activates an offer/price; a price accepted at purchase is not modified retroactively → the Backend derives the Parent-account or organization scope from the verified principal/assignment and does not accept an owner ID chosen by the client → Billing returns the quota summary and active offers/price versions → the Parent or School Manager selects a suitable plan and a manual or auto-renew payment method if supported by the provider → the purchase command creates an invoice/payment attempt → the MoMo/VNPAY callback is verified, deduplicated, and settled → Billing issues a grant → the School Manager may allocate a limit from the school's available quota to a Teacher who belongs to the school, or revoke the unused portion to return it to the school pool → usage/allocations/purchases are reconciled through the ledger. |
| Alternative flow | A pending/failed/unverifiable payment does not issue a grant; a duplicate callback is deduplicated; checkout is rejected if there is no active offer or the provider is not configured. A Teacher cannot purchase on their own; a School Manager outside the organization, an allocation exceeding the balance, or a Teacher outside the organization is rejected. Reclaiming only frees unused quota. Canceling auto-renew prevents future charges and does not create a refund. A request with exhausted quota is rejected with `VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`, creating no usage/job; existing videos and history can still be viewed. A request already accepted while quota remained consumes one unit even if generation subsequently fails due to a provider/technical error or is blocked by safety. |
| Postconditions | Billing computes quota from append-only grants minus the append-only usage ledger; there is no mutable balance. Unused included grants expire at the end of the month; a Parent who starts mid-month receives the full included amount per Q121. Top-ups do not expire until used up, per BR-38/Rev71. Within each month, included quota is used first, then top-ups in FIFO order (Q117–Q118, Q124–Q126); a Parent purchases only for their own account, and a School Manager purchases only for the assigned organization; a school allocation does not change the owner scope; an unused Teacher allocation is automatically returned to the School when the Teacher leaves the organization and does not restore usage already consumed. |
| Data/requirements | billing.video_quota_grants/video_quota_usage/invoices/payment_attempts/provider_event_receipts; ai_policy.resource_reservation/resource_event only for GPU/compute accounting; FR-B-10–13, BR-38 |

### UC-11 — An adult cancels an incomplete run

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The Parent owner or the Teacher currently assigned to the class; the former creator remains only as provenance |
| Prerequisites | The run is not in COMPLETED/FAILED/ABANDONED/DELETE_PENDING/DELETED; the caller still has permission under the current assignment; the current run revision is known |
| Trigger | The adult confirms Cancel run |
| Main flow | The application command checks actor/state/revision and idempotency → in a single transaction, moves the run to DELETE_PENDING and releases the active lock → revokes playback/upload → records a cancellation notice and a lifecycle request → sends a best-effort cancellation to the job/provider → the lifecycle deletes the linked content → records DELETED and minimal audit metadata |
| Alternative flow | If the provider is running, the system does not wait for the interrupt to succeed before releasing the lock; a late result becomes stale, not playable, and is purged. A video request that has already been accepted still retains its quota usage. A Teacher sample claim that has already succeeded remains on the occurrence even if the media is deleted. Repeated calls with the same idempotency key return the same result. |
| Postconditions | The run no longer holds the active lock as soon as the command is accepted; media/private content is deleted according to the lifecycle; consumed quota and the Teacher sample claim are not refunded. |
| Data/requirements | story.runs; be1.data_lifecycle_requests; ai_jobs.cancellation_notice; core.asset_metadata/lineage; FR-C-08, BR-26, BR-40 |

### UC-12 — A School Manager changes the responsible Teacher and offboards

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The School Manager of an organization who has been granted class management permission |
| Prerequisites | The active class has a current Teacher; the replacement belongs to the organization/is provisioned. A normal transfer is accepted only if there is no active-run lock conflict; an emergency force-close is explicitly performed by the School Manager before retry. |
| Trigger | The School Manager chooses to change the Teacher or offboard the current Teacher |
| Main flow | The Backend preflights permission/assignment version → BE1 records transfer-pending and revokes the old access/token immediately, and the class becomes read-only → the Story owner-transfer command checks uniqueness and moves `active_owner_teacher_id`/the lock within the Story transaction → BE1 finalizes the current assignment after the receipt → the new Teacher is granted access; creator provenance is retained; audit metadata/inbox are recorded. Valid jobs continue, and completion is accepted only according to the current input/hash/occurrence/assignment fence. |
| Alternative flow | If the new Teacher has an active run, Story returns a conflict; the saga compensates BE1 back to the old assignment and restores the old access if verified. If the compensating operation times out or is ambiguous, the system keeps transfer-pending/read-only and reconciles with the same idempotency key; no content is granted to the old/new Teacher before the state is consistent. The School Manager of the organization that owns the class may force-close the conflicting run via an explicit emergency Story command with a reason/audit metadata; the status becomes ABANDONED, the lock is released, and media is neither deleted nor made viewable to the School Manager. The School Manager then retries the reassignment. Quota/sample claim remain unchanged; authorization persistence/resolver remain open. |
| Postconditions | On finalization, the class has exactly one current Teacher; `created_by_actor_ref` is unchanged; the new access is opened only after the lock receipt; admins cannot view media. A pending transfer does not allow creating a run. |
| Data/requirements | be1.organization_assignments/classrooms; story.runs(active owner); core.audit_events; notifications.inbox_items; FR-T-02/14/17, BR-05/06/41; Q130/Q134 |

### UC-13 — A School Manager provisions a Teacher and recovers an adult account

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The School Manager of the organization and the adult who needs to activate/recover an account |
| Prerequisites | The identity verification channel is configured; the School Manager is authenticated; the Parent/Teacher scope has not been disclosed through public lookup |
| Trigger | The School Manager invites a Teacher, or an adult initiates account recovery |
| Main flow | The Backend first creates/ensures the Firebase Authentication identity, records the BE1 identity in a pending-verification state with an assignment that is not yet active → the Firebase Admin SDK creates an email-verification/password-reset action link → the notification email adapter sends the link → the recipient verifies and completes sign-in → the backend verifies the Firebase ID token together with `email_verified`, then activates the scope/assignment according to policy → returns a generic result that does not disclose a UID in another scope. Firebase manages the action code; the application stores only the identity/assignment state and adds no invitation-token table. |
| Alternative flow | An expired/used link returns a generic status and allows the admin to resend; a Parent/Teacher account conflict returns a generic `ACCOUNT_SETUP_UNAVAILABLE`; an email provider failure keeps the invitation pending with a finite number of retries; if Firebase Auth cannot issue/check the action link, the account is not activated and the request moves to a retryable state. |
| Postconditions | There is no client-self-granted role claim; the audit stores metadata; no action token or raw recipient email is stored in the app database/logs; the provider-managed activation link is single-use/expiring. |
| Data/requirements | be1.adult_identities/organization_assignments; core.audit_events; notifications; Firebase Authentication action-link adapter; no token table; FR-C-01, FR-O-09 |

### UC-14 — Content administration publishes active catalog activities

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | Platform Admin (Content Admin permission) |
| Prerequisites | The actor has catalog permission; the safety/content checklist and catalog schema version are in effect |
| Trigger | Create, approve, publish, edit, or retire an outdoor activity |
| Main flow | Create an immutable catalog revision → validate age/context/accessibility/safety metadata → a different reviewer or an independent review permission approves → publish with an effective time → a Parent receives only the published version; the Teacher activity snapshot pins the exact version |
| Alternative flow | If validation/safety fails, the revision stays in draft/rejected and is not visible in the Parent catalog; an edit creates a new revision; retiring does not change the snapshot of runs already created |
| Postconditions | The catalog has provenance, revisions, and audit; a Teacher-only activity remains only within the occurrence and does not automatically become a global activity |
| Data/requirements | learning_content.records/versions/reviews/events/outbox; FR-P-05, FR-T-09, BR-08, BR-36 |

### UC-15 — Register, revoke, and deliver push notifications

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | A Parent/Teacher registers the endpoint on the client; the Notifications worker sends |
| Prerequisites | The actor is authenticated; notification permission has been granted by the user; the endpoint/provider is supported on the client |
| Trigger | Login/enabling notifications, logout, permission revocation, or the provider reports an invalid endpoint |
| Main flow | The client sends the endpoint to the API over TLS → the backend checks actor/platform/provider and encrypts the endpoint → stores a fingerprint to prevent duplicates → creates the inbox notice first → dispatches the push on a best-effort basis with a minimal payload → records the delivery attempt/status |
| Alternative flow | If the push is rejected, the endpoint is invalid, the Android provider is not yet supported, or delivery fails, the inbox is still stored; the endpoint is revoked and there is no infinite retry; no child name/media/playback URL is sent |
| Postconditions | The inbox is the authoritative source, and the push does not change business state; the endpoint can be revoked and the token is not exposed in logs |
| Data/requirements | notifications.push_endpoints, inbox_items, delivery_attempts; FR-B-09/14, NFR-26, Q131 |

### UC-16 — Quota exhausted, purchase a top-up, and submit feedback on an inappropriate video

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The Parent or the currently responsible Teacher |
| Prerequisites | The actor has run permission; video generation has not exceeded the limit; the quota scope is determined from the identity/organization |
| Trigger | The user starts/regenerates a video, quota is exhausted, or the user flags a video as inappropriate |
| Main flow | The UI displays the quota estimate/remaining requests before accepting a full-video request → idempotent Billing admission records one usage unit before dispatch → when quota is exhausted, displays the reason/period and a purchase button if the actor has permission → after verified payment, the user actively presses retry; when flagging as inappropriate, the current video is hidden and the run returns to script review before a new generation |
| Alternative flow | If rejected before admission, no quota is consumed; if the request has been accepted and then fails with a technical/provider/safety error, the unit is not refunded per Q128; existing playback is retained when quota is exhausted, but a video rejected by the user remains hidden; no automatic retry after quota is granted |
| Postconditions | No generation is dispatched if quota is exhausted; the quota usage ledger contains no child content; quality feedback does not automatically grant operators permission to view the video |
| Data/requirements | billing.video_quota_grants/video_quota_usage; story.video_generations/review_decisions; notifications; FR-B-10–14, FR-P-09, FR-T-12–16, BR-11/16/38 |

### UC-17 — A Teacher creates and presents an AI Sketch Deck based on a lesson plan

| Item | Specification |
|---|---|
| Primary actor | The Teacher responsible for the occurrence |
| Prerequisites | The occurrence belongs to an assigned class; the plan/activity has been approved. The Teacher enables the line-sketch guide in a newly created session for the job; if not enabled, the use case does not run. The age band is in the plan or confirmed by the Teacher; the sketch rate/cost budget is still available. |
| Trigger | The Teacher enables the line-sketch guide, opens a READY deck for presentation, or requests regeneration of a sketch. Disabled/skip before generation creates no job/deck/asset. |
| Main flow | When the Teacher enables the guide, the backend pins the approved plan revision/hash and creates a deck revision → each guide, per activity/subject, has an adaptive progressive sequence of 4–8 steps; the SVG catalog is used first, and an AI draft only when the capability is permitted → the backend checks schema/coverage/safety/media/lineage and then marks it READY → the Teacher previews/controls Back/Next/select/skip/regenerate and presents on a white 16:9 background. The Teacher may edit the text/order/slides and place an uploaded image, an SVG, or a validated YouTube/Vimeo link into a slide in the presentation revision → the children draw on paper → in the existing video flow, the Teacher selects one activity and a randomly chosen eligible representative child, captures the drawing, and runs a new story-video → the video appears only after the pipeline/safety gate. |
| Alternative flow | If the Teacher does not enable it, the occurrence continues without a deck/job. If the plan changes, the old deck becomes STALE; regenerate creates a new immutable revision. A failed sketch may be skipped/regenerated; when the cost/rate cap is exhausted, a sketch-specific error is returned and no video quota is consumed. |
| Postconditions | The occurrence has one READY deck revision tied to the current plan hash, or still has no deck; Back/Next/skip state is not stored on the server. The deck is shared and does not carry the child's profile/drawing/identity. The sample story video still has only one child and one activity/occurrence. |
| Data/requirements | lesson.sketch_deck_revisions/steps; lesson.plan_revisions/activities; core.asset_metadata/asset_lineage; ai_jobs.job/attempt; AI Policy compute metering; FR-T-18–20, FR-AI-14, BR-42–44, NFR-33 |

**Separate support feedback:** V1 has defined marking a video as inappropriate to hide it and return to the script. The flow for submitting a support report/attaching a video for the operator is not yet defined; by default, operators are not granted permission to read media, and report media is not collected until access permission, consent/authorization, and retention are approved.

<a id="srs-20"></a>
## 20. Authorization matrix

The product roles in the matrix are Parent, Teacher, School Manager, and Platform Admin as in §31.3. School Manager is the unified name for the permission to manage one organization (historical name "Organization Admin"). Content review/publish/moderation is a permission capability within Platform Admin, not a separate role. System Operator is a technical principal, not a product role. A Teacher may read runs/media for the currently assigned class; after a reassignment, the permission transfers once the assignment commits and the former Teacher's permission/tokens are revoked immediately; `created_by` is provenance only. School Manager and Platform Admin do not have child media read permission by default. Platform Admin child-media access is deny until the owner approves a separate access process; a standalone reason/audit does not create permission. System Operator handles only redacted health/metadata and lifecycle commands, and does not read content.

| Resource/operation | Parent | Responsible Teacher | School Manager | Platform Admin (with required permission) | System Operator |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Create/read/update own Parent profile | Own profile | No | No | No | No |
| Read/update Parent support-needs notes | Own profile only | No | No | Deny by default; child-media/profile access is not yet opened | No |
| Create/read managed Teacher profile | No | Assigned classes only | Roster within own organization | No | No |
| Manage classes/Teacher assignments | No | No | Own organization | Manages platform policy, does not replace School Manager | No |
| Manage roster members | No | Assigned classes per policy | Own organization | No | No |
| Read lesson documents/plans | No | Teacher responsible for the occurrence | Metadata/lesson content review within the organization per permission | Public/catalog lessons per content permission | No content permission |
| Create/read/present/regenerate AI Sketch Deck | No | Teacher responsible for the occurrence | Metadata; no child/deck media content by default | No | No content permission |
| Select Parent activity | Published catalog only | Published catalog only | Published catalog only | Review/publish when having content permission | No |
| Create custom activity for Teacher occurrence | No | Responsible Teacher | No | No | No |
| Create story run | Own Parent profile | One child in the assigned class/approved occurrence | No | No | No |
| Read story/script/transcript/video | Own Parent run | Runs of the currently assigned class, including runs created before reassignment | Deny | Deny until the owner-approved process; the role does not self-grant permission | No content permission |
| Support access to child media | Not applicable | Assigned runs only | Deny | Deny-by-default; permission/approval/expiry/break-glass process remains open | Deny |
| Mark ready video as inappropriate/regenerate | Own Parent run | Run of the currently assigned class, if the run still permits the operation | No | No | No |
| Confirm showing video to the class in an occurrence | No | Current responsible Teacher; confirmation is recorded per occurrence | No | No | No |
| View quota summary | Own Parent account scope | School pool of the assigned occurrence | School pool of own organization | Aggregated platform policy only | Aggregated operational metrics only |
| Purchase/allocate video quota | Parent purchases monthly/top-up for own account | Cannot purchase; uses only the organization limit per occurrence | School Manager purchases for the organization and allocates/reclaims the unused portion of Teacher allocation within the organization | Platform Admin creates/versions offers and prices, does not purchase on behalf of customers | Provider configuration only |
| Delete run content in COMPLETED, FAILED, or ABANDONED | Own Parent run | Run of the currently assigned class, per lifecycle | No | No | Lifecycle command only, does not read content |
| Cancel an incomplete run | Own Parent run | Run of the currently assigned class, per lifecycle | No | No | Lifecycle command only, does not read content |
| Manage subscription/payment/capacity | No | No | Has billing permission within own organization | Platform policy | No |
| View system health/metrics with sensitive information removed | No | No | No | Per platform administration permission | Yes |

The backend checks role and permission on every read/write operation, on issuing playback tokens, and on asynchronous completion. A role claim from the UI is only a hint; the backend loads and verifies the resource scope together with the current assignment. Content permission does not imply child-media permission. A denial response must not reveal whether another organization's profile/run exists.

<a id="srs-21"></a>
## 21. API details and client interaction

### 21.1 Common HTTP rules

- All production requests use HTTPS. Local Compose may use HTTP only on loopback/the development network.
- The base path is versioned, starting from /v1. Incompatible contract changes require a new major path/schema version and a compatibility period; optional additional fields may be released within the current version.
- The backend returns a correlation ID for the request in every response. The client sends an idempotency key for state-changing commands and keeps the same key when retrying the same logical command.
- The Firebase ID token is sent in the Authorization header. The backend verifies the issuer, audience, expiry, and signature before deriving the principal. Do not trust role, owner ID, organization ID, or permission fields supplied by the request body.
- JSON request bodies are bounded. Binary uploads use a session/chunk API that checks media type, byte size, and checksum; do not send large bytes in JSON commands, and do not issue MinIO/S3 credentials or endpoints to the client. Reject unknown fields for security-sensitive commands, or ignore them only when a versioned contract explicitly allows compatible extension in the future.
- Server timestamps are in UTC ISO-8601. Dates are displayed per the configured timezone of the organization/user; the proposed default timezone for the billing UI is Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh and it must be configurable.
- List APIs use cursor pagination with a stable opaque cursor, a default page size of 25, and a maximum of 100. The sort order must be explicit and deterministic; do not use SQL field names sent by the client.
- Every response is a versioned resource/command result or the standard error envelope in §9.8. Do not serialize ORM entities or raw provider responses.

### 21.2 Resource contract outline

| Method and path | Actor | Request structure/invariants | Success response |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST /v1/parent/profiles | Parent | Bounded profile fields; no organization ID | 201 profile ref + revision |
| GET/PATCH /v1/parent/profiles/{profile_id} | Owning Parent | PATCH has expected revision; creates a new immutable revision | 200 current profile projection/revision |
| POST /v1/organizations/{org_id}/classes | School Manager of the organization | Check effective capacity within the transaction | 201 class ref or CLASS_CAPACITY_REACHED |
| POST /v1/classes/{class_id}/assignments | School Manager of the organization | Exactly one active responsible Teacher | 200 assignment revision |
| POST /v1/classes/{class_id}/profiles | Assigned Teacher | Structured profile fields and expected class revision; one active enrollment | 201 profile/enrollment ref |
| POST /v1/lesson-occurrences | Assigned Teacher | Class ref, occurrence label/date; verify capacity/assignment | 201 occurrence ref |
| POST /v1/lesson-occurrences/{id}/documents | Assigned Teacher | Attach asset ref from a completed upload session; only PDF/PPTX/DOCX ≤25 MB/100 pages/slides; source hash verified by the server | 202 extraction job + source ref |
| POST /v1/upload-sessions | Authorized Parent or Teacher | Purpose, media type, expected byte size/hash, scoped target ref, and idempotency key; the server derives actor/scope | 201 upload session ref, chunk size (≤4 MiB), accepted-part set, expiry; no MinIO URL/credential returned; Core tables `upload_sessions`/`upload_parts` own resume state |
| GET /v1/upload-sessions/{upload_id} | Session-owning actor | Same actor/scope only and session still valid | 200 state, chunk receipts/bitmap, byte count, and expiry |
| PUT /v1/upload-sessions/{upload_id}/parts/{part_no} | Session-owning actor | Raw chunk ≤4 MiB configured, part number, SHA-256; same part/hash retry is idempotent | 200 part receipt and progress; duplicate part number with a different hash returns 409 |
| POST /v1/upload-sessions/{upload_id}/complete | Session-owning actor | Expected aggregate size/hash; purpose/scope cannot be modified | 202 complete/validation state; asset ref is usable only when hash, media validation, and AV scan pass |
| DELETE /v1/upload-sessions/{upload_id} | Session-owning actor | Expected state/version; idempotent | 202 abort/purge state; partial bytes must not be used |
| POST /v1/lesson-occurrences/{id}/plan-revisions | Responsible Teacher | Document revision, objectives, story direction, and list of activities | 201 immutable draft plan revision |
| POST /v1/lesson-occurrences/{id}/plan-revisions/{revision}/confirm | Responsible Teacher | Expected plan hash and safety checklist for custom activities | 200 approved revision |
| POST /v1/lesson-occurrences/{id}/projection-confirmation | Responsible Teacher | Approved occurrence and current sample that has passed safety checks; explicit confirmation of audience/use; idempotency key | 200 confirmation receipt per occurrence |
| POST /v1/lesson-occurrences/{id}/projection-playback-token | Responsible Teacher | Current occurrence confirmation, selected sample, current assignment version | 200 short-lived token for the Teacher to control projection to the class |
| POST /v1/story-runs | Parent or Teacher | Exactly one typed profile ref; scope matches role; optional typed lesson-version ref; Parent includes exactly one main activity selection/ref chosen before the script; Teacher includes occurrence/plan and exactly one approved activity; idempotency key | 201 run resource; does not accept an array of children |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/assets | Run actor | Attach one drawing/audio asset that has passed upload validation; asset owner must match the run | 201/202 asset ref and validation state |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/idea | Run actor | Text, audio ref, or both; audio limits; expected run revision | 202 input/ASR job refs |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/subject-confirmation | Run actor | Confirmed candidate or limited edits; expected VLM revision | 202 confirmation accepted or VLM rerun job |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/context-confirmation | Run actor | Snapshot version/hash and explicit adult confirmation | 200 pinned snapshot ref |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/story-rewrites | Run actor | Optional limited and screened suggestions; expected story revision Recovery from `FAILED`: Parent only, under the §6.1.1 conditions; otherwise 409 `PARENT_STORY_REWRITE_NOT_ALLOWED`. | 202 rewrite job or 409 rewrite limit exceeded |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/script-confirmation | Run actor | Exact script/storyboard revision/hash | 200 confirmation receipt; old revision returns 409 |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/video-generations | Run actor | Current confirmed script hash; quality-reason code when regenerating; idempotency key | 202 intent ref; state `ADMISSION_PENDING` until both receipts are clear. When accepted, state `ACCEPTED`/job ref only after budget reservation + quota admission. Quota denial returns 409 `VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`; cost denial returns 503 `AI_COST_LIMIT_REACHED`; a rejected intent creates no usage/job/dispatch/cap |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/video-unsuitable | Run actor | Current generation ID and safe reason code | 200 run returns to script review or limit error |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/complete | Run actor | Valid only in VIDEO_READY; current run revision; idempotency key | 200 COMPLETED state ends the run and releases the active lock; not a video approval |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/cancel | Run actor | Run not yet completed; current run revision; idempotency key | 202 DELETE_PENDING state; releases the active lock within the transaction and starts job cancellation/purge lifecycle |
| DELETE /v1/story-runs/{id} | Parent owner or authorized Teacher | Only runs in COMPLETED, FAILED or ABANDONED; idempotent lifecycle command | 202 deletion request/status |
| GET /v1/jobs/{job_id} | Authorized run actor | Job must belong to the actor's scope | 200 versioned job state |
| POST /v1/story-runs/{id}/playback-token | Currently authorized run actor | Current artifact in VIDEO_READY, passed checks; check current assignment/scope | 200 short-lived playback token, limited to one asset; run active until complete, idle closeout or cancel |
| POST /v1/organizations/{org_id}/subscription/checkout | Billing admin | Price version, billing period and desired capacity | 202 payment attempt/redirect instruction |
| GET /v1/video-quota | Parent owner, assigned Teacher or organization billing admin | Derive scope from the verified actor; do not accept owner ID from the client | 200 quota granted/used/available and calculated period boundary |
| POST /v1/video-quota/purchases (TO-BE) | Parent account owner or School Manager with permission in the organization | Active offer, scope from principal, amount/price version determined by the server, idempotency key | Creates invoice/payment attempt; grants only after verified settlement |
| POST /v1/payments/{provider}/callback | Provider | Signed provider payload; verify replay/idempotency | Separate provider acknowledgement; does not use browser auth |
| GET /v1/notifications | Adult receiving notifications | Cursor pagination | 200 inbox items containing no child media/story |
| POST /v1/notifications/push-endpoints | Authenticated adult | Platform/provider type, encrypted endpoint registration proof, idempotency key | 201 endpoint ref; raw endpoint is not logged |
| DELETE /v1/notifications/push-endpoints/{endpoint_id} | Endpoint owner | Actor scope, expected endpoint version | 204 revoked; does not affect inbox |

The route shapes are a contract to be finalized in OpenAPI. In particular, the story run creation body must represent a single profile field; children[], profiles[], participant_ids[] or a class field must absolutely not be used as the subject.

### 21.3 Upload and playback security

- Upload in the local demo may go through the API or use a single-object upload grant with a narrow scope and an expiry time. The grant points to exactly one opaque asset key, the expected content type and the maximum byte size. This is not a shared MinIO credential.
- After upload, the server verifies the hash, size and content signature of the object; an asset that is not completed/not verified must not be used in an AI job.
- The playback token is short-lived, limited to one ready video and actor/run, and is re-checked against current Parent ownership or Teacher assignment. Revoking a Teacher assignment immediately prevents issuing new tokens.
- Do not expose bucket names, object keys, permanent URLs or listing APIs to the client. Do not provide export/download functionality. Stream video only through the authorized playback route/token.
- Prevent reuse of a token outside its validity period or for unauthorized resources. Avoid writing tokens to app logs, analytics, the clipboard or error reports.

### 21.4 Idempotency, concurrency and optimistic concurrency

- State-changing commands must have an idempotency key per principal, operation and target resource. Resending the same key/payload returns the original result; reusing a key with a different payload returns INVALID_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY.
- Run creation uses a durable uniqueness rule and a transaction. Two concurrent requests from the same adult create one run; the other request receives ACTIVE_RUN_EXISTS.
- Changes to profile, lesson plan, story and script must send the expected revision/hash. A stale request receives STALE_REVISION along with the current version, and does not overwrite the current head.
- Provider callbacks use the provider/event ID and signature verification, not the client's idempotency token.
- Recompute the payment amount/currency and desired capacity from the immutable server-managed price version. Never treat a total sent by the client as the authoritative source.

### 21.5 Client screen catalog

| App/area | Required screens or surfaces | Main behavior |
|---|---|---|
| Android Parent | Sign-in/account recovery; child profile list/form; run history; capture/select drawing; text/audio idea; transcript review; subject confirmation/editing; activity catalog; context snapshot review; script/storyboard review; job progress; video player; Complete/cancel run; quota summary; buy monthly quota plan or one-time top-up, choose manual payment or authorized auto-renew; delete runs in COMPLETED, FAILED or ABANDONED | Contextual permission requests; denying camera still allows uploading a photo from the gallery; denying microphone still allows entering an idea as text; stages continue after the app goes to the background; quota state comes from the server; no child batch/gallery/export |
| Web Teacher | Sign-in; select organization/class; roster; structured profile form; lesson occurrence; document upload/extraction; review/edit extracted text revision; plan/objective/story direction/activity checklist; view/confirm the child randomly selected by the system under the approved policy; camera/upload; review transcript/subject/context; review script; job progress; video player; confirm screening per occurrence; Complete/cancel run; view the school quota of the occurrence (no Teacher-account pool, no separate quota purchase action); run/occurrence history | Show only assigned classes; the safety checklist blocks plan approval; the occurrence shows the status of one sample video; screening requires confirmation; no bulk creation per roster |
| School Manager | Organization dashboard; view class capacity; create/archive/suspend classes; assign responsible Teachers; roster administration; billing period/invoice/payment status; auto-renew/downgrade class plans; view school quota; buy quota plans for the organization; allocate limits and reclaim unused Teacher portions; notification inbox | When creating a class, check the effective capacity; downgrading requires selecting classes; the billing screen never exposes child media/story |
| Platform Admin — Content management | Draft/review/publish activity catalog | A published version is immutable; changes create a new version that requires review |
| Platform Admin — Quota plans and pricing | Create, version, activate/discontinue Parent/School monthly offers and one-time top-ups; view price history/purchases and reconciliation metadata | Do not change the price of a settled purchase; do not read child profile/media; every change is audited |
| Dashboard operator | Health, queue, aggregated job state, billing reconciliation, lifecycle backlog | Only sanitized operational metadata; no drill-down into profile/story/media |

The run screen explains when adult input is needed. Do not display fabricated percentages. Neither the child nor the adult can approve merely by moving through the screens; confirmation must be a command bound to the exact revision hash.

### 21.6 Client recovery behavior by error group

| Error group/code | Client behavior | Retry policy |
|---|---|---|
| AUTH_REQUIRED, INVALID_TOKEN, TOKEN_EXPIRED | Refresh or re-authenticate with Firebase; return to the original screen only after the token is verified | Retry once after a successful refresh; do not loop indefinitely |
| RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND (ID does not exist or is out of scope) | Report not found/inaccessible in the same way; refresh the list of permitted resources | Do not retry the same resource command |
| ACTION_NOT_ALLOWED (resource in the same scope but the actor is not permitted to perform the action) | Report that the action is not allowed, without revealing internal policy | Do not retry the same command; continue only when the role/assignment is valid |
| ACTIVE_RUN_EXISTS | Offer to continue/open the current active run if the caller owns it, or cancel the unfinished run; do not create a second run | Retry creation only after complete, cancellation is accepted, or the run has failed/ended |
| MULTI_CHILD_NOT_SUPPORTED | Explain that each story is tied to one system-selected child; return to the Teacher step of viewing/confirming the child/context | Do not retry automatically |
| STALE_REVISION | Reload the current revision and ask the adult to review/confirm again | Retry only with the new expected revision |
| OCR_NOT_SUPPORTED, UNSUPPORTED_LESSON_TYPE, LESSON_FILE_TOO_LARGE | Explain the supported PDF/PPTX/DOCX text and the limits; allow replacing the file or manually editing text if supported | Do not retry blindly |
| TRANSCRIPT_NOT_CONFIRMED, SUBJECT_NOT_CONFIRMED, PROFILE_CONTEXT_NOT_CONFIRMED | Return to the corresponding review screen and keep the in-progress edit state | Continue only after explicit confirmation |
| LESSON_PLAN_NOT_APPROVED | Send the Teacher back to the occurrence plan review step | Do not start a child run/job |
| STORY_REWRITE_LIMIT_REACHED | Keep the current story; let the adult confirm the story or stop the run | Do not automatically create a new story job |
| VIDEO_REGENERATION_LIMIT_REACHED | Report that regeneration attempts for quality improvement are exhausted; playback follows the current generation state; output marked unsuitable remains hidden from playback | Do not retry automatically |
| VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED | Show the remaining quota summary/period and the permitted options to buy more; keep current playback/history | Do not retry generation; retry only after the grant is verified |
| STORY_OUTPUT_INVALID | Report that the script failed the check; the Parent may rewrite once under BR-13 | No automatic retry |
| AI_COST_LIMIT_REACHED before admission | Explain that the system cannot temporarily accept the request; do not display it as a provider/model error and confirm that no quota was consumed | The same idempotency key returns the same rejection; after the budget/circuit breaker allows it, the adult actively presses again to create a new command with a new key |
| PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE | Show a safe temporary error with the correlation ID and retry guidance | Retry only if the job state allows it; keep the idempotency key |
| AWAITING_RECONCILIATION | Show “Đang kiểm tra trạng thái tạo”; do not create another video job | Poll the job state until a result is available |
| SUBSCRIPTION_RESTRICTED / CLASS_CAPACITY_REACHED | Link to the permitted billing/capacity action; keep current data | Retry after verifying the payment/capacity change |
| RATE_LIMITED | Show brief wait guidance | Wait per Retry-After; do not repeat requests continuously |
| Lifecycle state DELETE_PENDING | Show that deletion is in progress and block playback/new runs on the deleted profile | Poll the lifecycle state; the server retries object deletion |

The client never displays raw provider codes, SQL errors, stack traces, filesystem paths or exception content.

### 21.7 External provider communication contract

The following structures are logical adapter contracts for checking boundaries and idempotency; they are not the wire-format payloads of MoMo/VNPAY or Modal. Detailed payloads/headers/signatures may be finalized only from the official documentation and the sandbox of the correct merchant/account.

| Adapter contract | Minimum request | Minimum response/event | Mandatory controls |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PaymentCallbackV1` | Provider kind, raw-body digest, provider event ID, merchant/payment ref, amount/currency, provider status, event time, received time, signature verification outcome | Acknowledge in the provider's format after durable receipt; canonical payment state/event ref | Verify the signature on the exact bytes/algorithm per provider; freshness/replay; unique event ID; check amount/currency/order; idempotent callback; browser redirect is not authoritative; secret only in the backend |
| `ModalVideoJobRequestV1` | Opaque job/run ref, idempotency key, model/profile revision, config/policy version, one-child source manifest refs + hashes, output scratch prefix, media/output bounds, deadline, cancellation ref | Durable `FunctionCall` ref; state/result metadata; output refs/checksum/media type/byte size; provider error class | Do not pass secrets or large free-form child payloads through function args; private job scratch; worker exits after spawn; poll/reconcile does not hold an RQ worker; verify hash/schema/format; timeout/cancel/stale cleanup |
| `ModalStageResultV1` | FunctionCall ref, expected job/input/config hashes | `SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELLED/UNKNOWN`, output manifest, checksum, duration/GPU seconds, provider version | UNKNOWN goes to reconciliation, do not blindly call the GPU repeatedly; late-arriving output is stale; only backend ingestion grants playback permission |
| `NotificationDeliveryV1` | Recipient actor ref, endpoint ref, notice type, dedupe key, expiry | Accepted/delivered/failed/invalid endpoint; provider receipt ref | Inbox is written first; bounded retry; minimal payload; raw endpoint encrypted; revoke invalid endpoint; push failure does not roll back business state |

**Wireframe/UI contract:** No graphical wireframes are created in this Markdown phase. Screens and interaction states are described in §5, §19, §21.5–21.6; before implementation, product/design sign-off is needed for the profile form, lesson extraction/review, story approval, quota exhaustion/top-up, class projection, cancellation/deletion, empty/error/loading states and accessibility flows. This is OI-17 in §14.1.

<a id="srs-22"></a>
