<a id="srs-31"></a>
## 31. Lesson plan platform, sharing and integration with the existing flow

### 31.1 Priority order and scope of change

This is a foundation layer added to the Sketch2Life product baseline. The target requirements for Parent Android, Teacher web, drawing capture, story/video pipeline, quota, safety, lifecycle and operations in §§1–30 continue to apply except for the points explicitly listed below. This is a requirements baseline and does not assert that the flows are complete in the current code. It does not replace the entire SRS or delete history.

This section is the v2.3 normative source for the changed points: age scope 36–120 months replacing 36–155 months; Teacher Sketch Deck and Parent Guide are both opt-in per session; reusable lessons are separated from occurrences; Parent displays outdoor activities after the video (Rev66: the main activity is selected before the script); Parent/Teacher profiles remain separate by owner but have sharing grants; and the system has the Platform Admin/School Manager roles. v2.2 sentences/matrices that contradict these points are treated as history and yield to the requirements with IDs at §31.8–§31.11.

This section does not lock the production stack, pricing, commercial quota or implementation schema. According to the historical record dated 2026-11-06, FLUX.3 was once the development direction for line-sketch/lesson visuals; the owner replaced this direction at Rev66 with an SVG catalog + deterministic vector engine. Rev75 (§31.21) re-admits one narrow exception for the optional drawing-guide picture of the Teacher Sketch Deck. Qwen structured vector draft is used only when the capability is enabled. Presenton remains a historical candidate for slide layout. Checkpoint, license review, safety, cost and backend connectivity still need verification. This entire section remains a draft; Plan Rev25 approves only document edits, not product code or migration.

### 31.2 Reusable lesson and occurrence model

The lesson library is a versioned content source that may contain:

- Topic, learning objectives, target age group and Montessori notes.
- Guidance for the child to draw the subject, the main strokes/features to observe, and information that helps the child understand that animal/object/topic.
- One or more outdoor activities; each states materials, preparation, execution steps, observation objectives and safety notes.
- Video script direction that may be switched on/off per lesson or per use; when on, the script is content guidance and does not require the videos for two children to be identical.
- The canonical content of a lesson is structured text. AI-generated visual assets/slides are a separate option, with version, provenance and review; they illustrate the lesson/activity or session, do not replace the source text and never replace the child's drawing image used for the video.

A reusable lesson version is independent of the lesson occurrence of a class/session. A Teacher or Parent uses a lesson as a preset; the occurrence, plan revision and StoryRun use the same optional typed `LessonVersionRefV1` with the exact version and content hash in the contract/snapshot when a preset is selected. A Teacher StoryRun further pins the exact lesson version/hash and approved plan revision/hash; a Parent StoryRun also pins the version/hash when using a preset. When no lesson is used, the refs are absent; `latest` is not resolved, and old occurrences/runs are not retargeted. The physical storage location and specific constraints await approved DDL. Platform Admin and Teacher may initiate AI suggestions for edits; Parent may only edit a draft/personal copy that has been granted permission, and does not initiate new suggestions on their own. Every edit creates a draft/version/copy with lineage and requires review by an authorized person; it does not edit the published source version backward or change old occurrences/runs. A Parent personal copy is reusable and PRIVATE, keeps source-version lineage; a deletion request has a 30-day recovery. The field allowlist, physical owner and retention after recovery remain open at §31.10(12).

Lesson visibility is PRIVATE, SCHOOL, SELECTED_AUDIENCE or PUBLIC. A Teacher lesson belongs to the school by default. SCHOOL is viewable only within the owning organization; SELECTED_AUDIENCE grants access to a Parent group or invited people; PUBLIC can be searched and used across the whole platform. A Teacher lesson becomes PUBLIC only after the School Manager approves. A public lesson authored by a Platform Admin may be published only after another Platform Admin with Content Admin permission reviews it independently. A Platform Admin may hide/disable content under platform permissions. A public lesson may be used and remixed; a remix always creates a new source item/version with provenance to the original lesson. On unpublish/revoke, neutral metadata/history is kept but library access is revoked; content reopens only after it is made public again or a new grant is issued.

When a lesson that has been used is then unpublished, the lesson version, occurrence and usage history are kept. The right to search/download/open lesson content from the library is removed; a Parent may access the lesson content again only when the lesson is made public again or shared again via a valid grant. Delivery history does not automatically restore library access.

### 31.3 Roles, tenant and profile sharing

| Role | Scope |
|---|---|
| Platform Admin | Platform-wide; configures policy/role/quota, creates public content, performs moderation/hide according to permission, and views and replies to school-owned feedback according to audit. A public lesson authored by a Platform Admin requires independent review by another Platform Admin who has Content Admin permission before publication. Child-media access is denied by default. |
| School Manager | One organization corresponds to one school; manages Teacher/Parent groups and school quota, approves Teacher-authored public lessons, and views/approves lessons and feedback within the school. Teacher-authored PUBLIC lessons require School Manager approval. Does not implicitly have permission to view children's images/videos. |
| Teacher | Assigned classes/profiles within the school; creates/edits lessons; uses lesson presets; enters lessons via template/form; turns sketch on/off within a session; creates a sample video for one child/run. |
| Parent | Separate Parent account; owns the child profile; may grant/revoke a profile share to a named Teacher; uses PUBLIC/granted lessons; views taught lessons according to the profile share; creates new videos for their child; submits lesson feedback. The Parent quota package is recorded as a temporary assumption in §31.7, not a production price. |

The permission to review/publish/moderate catalog/lesson content is managed in the Platform Admin policy; "Content Admin" is not a product role or a separate login principal. That permission does not include child profile/media permissions. Within the current SRS scope, the historical label "Organization Admin/organization admin" for tenant administration is normalized to School Manager of the organization that owns the resource; this is a role-name unification and does not fix how identity/assignment is stored, permission grant, scope resolver, or revoke/audit mapping.

Each school is one organization/tenant. The organization boundary is mandatory for SCHOOL lessons, SELECTED lessons, occurrence, Parent groups, feedback, quota, and audit. A platform-wide Admin may moderate, but the Manager/Teacher/Parent roles do not cross tenants.

Parent and Teacher do not share an account and do not transfer profile ownership. A Parent profile may have 0..n share grants; each grant links to exactly one opaque Managed Profile reference, and each Managed Profile has at most one active Parent link. The Parent invites a named Teacher; a grant in `INVITED` does not confer permission, and the invitee Teacher must accept before the grant becomes `ACTIVE`. A grant has a 90-day term; a Parent revoke takes effect immediately for new reads, tokens/cache/API, and jobs not yet accepted. A Delivery Recipient is an occurrence snapshot, not a new Parent profile/Managed Profile link. An already accepted story run retains the adult-approved input snapshot under the existing lifecycle. A cross-owner ref remains a typed opaque reference, with no name-based matching or cross-owner SQL FK. The exact field allowlist, physical owner/schema, consent artifact, renewal, and handling of school-wide/class invitations remain OPEN.

| Resource/action | Parent | Teacher | School Manager | Platform Admin |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Profile | Owner; grants consent, sends invitations, and revokes grants | Only fields granted after the Teacher accepts the invitation and for the assigned class | Does not read profile contents by default | Role does not grant permission to read profile/media; denied by default until a process approved by the owner exists |
| School lesson | Used when a school/group/invite grant is active | Creates/edits school lessons | Views/gives feedback; approves Teacher public publication | Moderation/hide, platform-wide |
| Public lesson | Search/use/download according to capability | Use/remix or create own version | Reviews Teacher publication | Author; independent review before publish; moderate |
| Occurrence and child media | Only own child/run | Only assigned child/run, grant still valid | Deny | Deny-by-default; no approved permission workflow yet |
| Emergency force-close conflicting Teacher run | Not granted in the current SRS | Not granted in the current SRS | The School Manager of the organization that owns the class/run is the target actor for an explicit command; how the permission is granted/stored and the resolver remain OPEN | Platform Admin (Content Admin permission) is not granted this through content permission; child media remains denied |
| Lesson feedback | Create and view own threads | View/reply if belonging to an assigned occurrence/class | View/reply within the school tenant | View/reply; output belongs only to the school thread |
| Quota | View Parent-account usage; buys packages/top-up for their own Parent account (Rev70) | Uses the occurrence's school quota; no Teacher-account pool | View/administrate school usage according to grant; buys quota for the assigned organization and grants or revokes internal Teacher limits (Rev70/Rev71) | Manages policy; permission to read child media must not be inferred from role policy |

Permission checks run server-side for every resource operation. Loss of assignment, share revoke, unpublish/hide, or leaving the organization must invalidate the related grant/cache/download/playback capability; permission is not inferred from a UI role claim.


#### Mapping logic and profile-sharing decisions

| Concept | Canonical owner | Logical reference | Cardinality/optionality | Authorization and history |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Parent Child Profile | Parent profile domain | Parent-owned profile reference | One Parent profile has 0..n grants; the link to a School Managed Profile is optional. | The Parent is the owner; not merged with the School Managed Profile. |
| School Managed Profile | BE1/School tenant | Opaque school-managed profile reference within the organization scope | Each Managed Profile has at most one active Parent link; enrollment/Teacher assignment follows the existing BE1 invariants. | Roster access requires role, organization, class assignment, and an active link/grant. |
| Delivery Recipient | Lesson delivery domain | Opaque recipient reference combined with the tenant/occurrence contract | A recipient is an occurrence-level snapshot; not used in place of the Parent/Managed Profile link. | History retains snapshot metadata; content access remains limited by the currently valid grant. |
| Profile Share Grant / Invitation | Grant owner not yet physically determined | Versioned opaque grant reference; sender/recipient/purpose/scope pass through the contract | Each grant points to exactly one Managed Profile; the named Teacher must accept; the term is 90 days. | Parent revoke is immediate; revoke blocks new reads/tokens and jobs not yet accepted. Physical schema, allowlist, renewal, and school-wide/class acceptance remain OPEN. |

Do not match by name, date of birth, or approximate attributes. Before creating a logical link, the application must check the invitation/grant version, tenant, authenticated recipient principal, purpose, field scope, and consent according to the selected policy. The screen lists only authenticated links. Every cross-owner link is an opaque typed reference/version through a port; cross-owner SQL FK is prohibited. The field allowlist, school-wide/class acceptance, renewal, revoke race, retained-history behavior, and physical mapping continue to be technical review gates at §31.10/§31.10.2.

### 31.4 Authoring, upload filtering, and AI assistance

Teacher authoring has a structured form and a standardized lesson-document template. The template states topic, age band, objectives, drawing steps, subject knowledge, activities, materials, instructions, safety notes, and optional script direction. Keep the v2.2 upload limits (PDF/PPTX/DOCX at most 25 MB/100 pages, no OCR of scans, reject encrypted/password-protected files) until a new decision is made.

Uploads go through quarantine/AV, a parser, and a deterministic field mapper. The original raw file together with its hash/provenance is kept private within the organization. Only allowlisted fields that have been parsed, validated, previewed by the Teacher, and edited/confirmed by the Teacher may create structured input for AI. Do not send the whole lesson plan, the full extracted text, embedded images, personal metadata, or the original file to AI. If the parser/filter/validation does not pass, stop and return a correctable error; do not silently send the raw document.

AI assistance in creating a lesson/activity/plan creates only a draft; an authorized person must review, edit, and publish. AI may be used to supplement outdoor activities when the catalog is insufficient; each suggestion must include objectives, materials, preparation, step-by-step instructions, and safety notes. Visual/slide generation is a separate, optional job with review/provenance/safety. Per Q159, Platform Admin and Teacher may initiate AI lesson/activity suggestions; a Parent may only edit a draft they have been granted permission for, must not initiate new suggestions, and must not publish a source version. This rule also applies when reusing a lesson: Platform Admin/Teacher initiate an edit suggestion for a draft/personal copy; a Parent only edits output they have been granted permission for. The source version is not overwritten.

### 31.5 Parent, Teacher, and video flows after adding the platform

Parent: topic or lesson presentation is optional; SVG guide is optional; taking a photo is mandatory → VLM recognizes/cross-checks against the topic or lesson → warns on mismatch so the topic can be changed or the photo retaken at most once → adult confirms subject/context → selects one main activity through catalog-first before the story; if the catalog is not suitable, the adult may choose from exactly three AI drafts and request a set of a different kind from the options already shown/rejected → confirms context → story/script review → new video via the current pipeline → private playback. No-pin returns the main activity pinned per run; lesson pin returns all activities of the exact version and marks the main activity. API/error recovery remains OPEN-TECHNICAL.

This Rev65 paragraph is kept for traceability, but the activity-timing rule has been replaced by Rev66 §31.17: the Parent selects one main activity before the story; no-pin displays the run-pinned activity after the video; lesson pin displays all activities of the exact version and marks the main activity. The catalog-first source/privacy rule continues to apply. A Parent may use a public lesson or a shared lesson; a reusable personal copy is PRIVATE, retains source-version lineage, and does not write to the shared source.

The Teacher target flow keeps the occurrence/sample video. A Teacher may create an occurrence from a lesson preset or a self-created approved plan; a lesson preset is optional. During teaching, a line-sketch is created only if the Teacher turns it on; off means no job is enqueued and no sketch quota is charged. The system selects one child with equal probability from the eligible set, checks that assignment/consent is still valid, allows at most one reselect if the candidate is stale, then stops when the set is empty. The Teacher views and confirms the child/context before any run/job/quota/claim exists; one occurrence has at most one successful sample video. The exact eligibility evidence, concurrency contract, and error/retry schema remain OPEN.

Every video is a new generation from the drawing image of the correct child and the approved profile context. Do not reuse another child's video or a shared video cache as a personal result. The lesson script may be turned on/off; if on, the story/video follows that direction but images/clips are newly generated, allowing differences according to the drawing/profile. If the VLM misrecognizes the subject, the system requires an adult to confirm before correcting the subject and requests suggestions again. The steps VLM → structured story → storyboard → adult script review → Character Bible/reference → LTX-2.5/Modal per scene → consistency → FFmpeg/TTS/subtitles → output safety → private playback are unchanged.

### 31.6 Parent lesson history and feedback

An invited/linked Parent of a profile may view lessons/occurrences taught to the child for 30 days from the first delivery/open, along with public/shared lessons for which permission is still granted. A Parent may view details according to permission and download permitted lesson content; child videos still follow the current playback rule, with no download/export opened. Revoke/unpublish retains neutral metadata but does not reopen lesson content; content is accessible again only when the lesson is made public again or a new grant exists.

A Parent may submit feedback tied to a lesson version/delivery and receive replies from the Teacher, School Manager, or Platform Admin. The feedback scope is limited to the relevant organization; other organizations must not see it. This is a feedback thread for discussing the lesson, not a general chat. Do not add completion marks/checklists, uploading drawing images to feedback, or sending child media with replies. A School Manager/Admin may view feedback and reply; a platform admin's reply is still displayed only within that school.

### 31.7 Quota and billing platform

The platform pays the provider costs. Billing keeps quota grants/usage in two separate scopes: the school organization and the Parent account. A Teacher uses the quota of the school organization that owns the occurrence; a Parent uses the quota of the Parent's account. Review assumption (not production price/config): Parent gets 2 included uses/month and a top-up of 5 uses at ₫249.000; School gets 30 included uses/month and a top-up of 50 uses at ₫1.990.000. Included quota is used first, top-ups are used FIFO; included quota expires at the end of the month and top-ups do not expire until used up, per BR-38/Rev71. A Teacher has no separate purchase/owner pool or cross-scope fallback; an assigned School Manager may issue a hard allocation from the available school quota to a Teacher belonging to the organization and may revoke the unused portion. When a Teacher leaves the organization, the unused allocation is automatically returned to the School, while consumed usage still belongs to the School. A Parent buys for the Parent account; a School Manager buys for the organization. The Platform Admin manages versioned offers/prices. The monthly plan supports manual payment or auto-renew with explicit authorization and the ability to cancel; a top-up is a one-time purchase only. MoMo/VNPAY are the payment methods. No quota refund after successful settlement. Initial production prices, renewal-failure behavior, the recovery UI after `VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`, the auto-renew cycle versus calendar-month grants, and provider settlement remain OPEN.

Video generation for a Teacher sample debits the quota of the school organization associated with the occurrence; a Parent creating a new video for a child debits the Parent account quota. Within each scope, the current month's included grant is used first, followed by top-ups FIFO; when quota runs out, new video requests are rejected until a valid grant exists. AI Policy tracks compute/cost reservation and does not replace Billing product quota. Quota sketch, image/slide generation and authoring jobs have separate accounting/rate limits per policy. The numbers/prices in the preceding paragraph are provisional values for review only; purchase rights, allocation, payment methods and the no-refund policy are recorded in §31.20; initial production prices, renewal failure and settlement remain open per §31.10. Top-ups do not expire until used up, and an unused Teacher allocation is returned to the School when the Teacher leaves the organization, per Rev71.

### 31.8 New functional requirements (FR-PL)

| ID | Priority | Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| FR-PL-01 | M | Provide platform Admin, School Manager, Teacher and Parent roles with scoped permissions, server-side authorization, audit and revoke. |
| FR-PL-02 | M | Each school is one organization/tenant; every school record has tenant scope and cannot be read across organizations. |
| FR-PL-03 | M | Separate the reusable lesson item/version from the occurrence; the version is pinned to the teaching/usage instance if a lesson exists. Platform Admin/Teacher may ask the AI to propose edits when reusing a lesson; a Parent may only edit a draft/personal copy that has been granted and cannot initiate a suggestion. Output has lineage, a review gate and does not modify the source version. |
| FR-PL-04 | M | Support visibility PRIVATE, SCHOOL, SELECTED_AUDIENCE and PUBLIC; support searching PUBLIC lessons and downloading authorized lesson content. |
| FR-PL-05 | M | A Teacher-authored lesson may be PUBLIC only after School Manager approval. A public lesson authored by a Platform Admin requires independent review by another Platform Admin with Content Admin permission. Admin moderation/hide and unpublish revoke library access but do not delete version/history metadata. |
| FR-PL-06 | M | Allow sharing a lesson with a Parent group or designated/invited people; every grant has a status/revoke and respects the tenant boundary. |
| FR-PL-07 | M | Allow a Parent to invite a named Teacher and share a child profile via a scoped grant with audit, a 90-day term and revoke; each Parent profile has 0..n grants, each grant links to one opaque Managed Profile ref, and each Managed Profile has at most one active Parent link. |
| FR-PL-08 | M | Return only the profile fields that are granted and allowed by policy; revoke takes effect immediately and blocks new reads/tokens/jobs not yet accepted. The Delivery Recipient is an occurrence snapshot, not a profile link. The exact field allowlist and the physical owner/schema remain open. |
| FR-PL-09 | M | The Teacher has a structured authoring form and a standardized lesson upload template; keep the current file limits until a new decision is made. |
| FR-PL-10 | M | The parser/filter produces a structured allowlist; the raw original, the full text of the lesson plan, embedded images and metadata must not be sent to the model. |
| FR-PL-11 | M | The Teacher previews/corrects/confirms the structured draft before AI generation; a wrong template or parse uncertainty returns an error and requires correction. |
| FR-PL-12 | M | AI-assisted lesson/activity/plan content is always a draft and requires an authorized person to review/edit/publish; it is never made public automatically. |
| FR-PL-13 | M | A lesson version supports drawing steps, subject-learning notes, age target, one or more activities with materials/instructions/notes, and optional script direction. |
| FR-PL-14 | M | Include the ability to create visual activity/topic assets or session slides by choice; record provenance/version/safety and do not choose a fixed provider. Creating assets in each lesson/session is optional. |
| FR-PL-15 | M | Create a Parent or Teacher line-sketch only when an adult enables it in the session; when disabled, there is no generation job. |
| FR-PL-16 | M | The Parent chooses one main activity before the story; photo capture is mandatory and the video pipeline is unchanged; after the video, show the selected main activity, plus the full list from the exactly pinned lesson version if there is one. |
| FR-PL-17 | M | Lesson script-following may be turned on/off per lesson/usage; the Parent preset keeps the lesson's script direction, the Teacher keeps the approved objective/activity/safety, and an adult confirms the script revision before the video. The default, override limits and revision-pin contract remain OPEN in §31.10. |
| FR-PL-18 | M | Do not reuse videos between children; each video uses the drawing image and the approved profile context of the correct child. |
| FR-PL-19 | M | On a subject mismatch, require the adult to confirm/correct before re-suggesting; do not silently change the subject. |
| FR-PL-20 | M | The system selects one child with equal probability from the set that is valid for eligibility/consent; the Teacher confirms the child/context; a stale candidate is re-selected at most once; an empty set stops with no fallback. Before confirmation, no run, job, quota usage or claim is created/accepted. Exact predicate, evidence, concurrency and response contracts remain open. |
| FR-PL-21 | M | A Parent views lessons taught to the linked child for 30 days counted from the first delivery/open; revoke/unpublish keeps only neutral metadata and does not reopen the content. |
| FR-PL-22 | M | A Parent downloads permitted lessons and sends lesson feedback; Teacher/Manager/Admin may view/reply within the school scope; there is no direct chat, completion toggle or drawing image in feedback. |
| FR-PL-23 | M | School groups and named recipients are used as the audience for lesson sharing; memberships and grants can be revoked. |
| FR-PL-24 | M | Billing separates school quota and Parent account quota; Parent buys for Parent account; School Manager buys for its organization and may internally allocate/reclaim unused school quota for assigned Teachers; unused Teacher allocation returns automatically to School when Teacher leaves the organization while consumed usage remains School usage; Teacher usage debits occurrence school quota, with no Teacher-owned purchase pool or cross-scope fallback; included is used first, top-up FIFO; included expires at the end of the month, top-up does not expire until used up, per BR-38/Rev71. Review assumption: Parent 2 included/month + top-up 5/₫249.000; School 30 included/month + top-up 50/₫1.990.000. Prices/units are provisional, not production configuration. Buyer, School Manager allocation authority, MoMo/VNPAY, manual/opt-in auto-renew, one-time top-up, unused-allocation return and top-up no-expiry are owner-selected in §31.20/Rev71; production price, renewal failure, UI recovery after `VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`, auto-renew timing against calendar-month grants and provider settlement remain OPEN. AI Policy owns only compute reservation. |
| FR-PL-25 | M | Limit new profiles/runs to 36–120 months, divided into three versioned bands: 36–71, 72–107 and 108–120 months; a profile/run keeps the age-band version it used. A profile aged 121–155 months must not receive new runs; a run accepted before the age reaches 121 months may complete based on the eligibility snapshot at accept time and the current authorization/safety/lifecycle guards. A legacy draft is read/delete only; a one-time notice is shown when detected and when the draft is opened. Editing/archiving drafts is not allowed under the current clarification. |

### 31.8.1 Additional platform business rules

| ID | Business rule |
|---|---|
| BR-47 | The lesson-plan platform is an addition; the Parent/Teacher flows and the video generation pipeline already finalized continue to apply, except for changes explicitly recorded by the owner in v2.3. |
| BR-48 | Parent/Teacher accounts and profile owners are separate; cross-role profile reads occur only through invitation, consent, an active scope grant, and can be revoked. |
| BR-49 | One school corresponds to one organization/tenant; lessons, feedback, quota and group membership are not exposed across tenants. |
| BR-50 | A reusable lesson version differs from an occurrence; use/reuse/remix does not modify the source version, and an old occurrence keeps its pinned version. AI-proposed edits create a draft/personal copy with lineage and require human review. |
| BR-51 | Visibility is PRIVATE/SCHOOL/SELECTED/PUBLIC; Teacher-authored PUBLIC needs School Manager approval; Platform Admin-authored PUBLIC needs independent review by another Platform Admin with Content Admin permission; an Admin may hide. |
| BR-52 | The original teacher lesson upload is kept separately; only the structured allowlist reviewed by the Teacher is allowed to go into the AI. |
| BR-53 | AI lesson/activity/visual outputs default to DRAFT; human review/publish is mandatory before public/show. |
| BR-54 | Parent and Teacher line-sketch run only when an adult enables them in the session; when off, there is no AI job. |
| BR-55 | The Parent must choose one main activity, catalog-first, after the VLM subject is confirmed by an adult; the backend finds candidates from PUBLIC/SHARED lesson versions that the Parent is permitted to view; an initial lesson pin is limited to the exact version/hash. Qwen3.5 chooses candidate IDs/reasons; if there is no suitable candidate, exactly 3 drafts may be created through a quality gate for the adult to choose from. Do not send raw images/audio/identifiers. The selected activity is story context and takes place after the video; requesting a different set of choices than those already shown/rejected is not allowed. After the video, no-pin returns the run-pinned activity; a lesson pin returns the full exact-version list and marks the main activity. API/snapshot/error mapping remains OPEN-TECHNICAL. |
| BR-56 | A video is always new based on the drawing of the correct child; do not reuse another child's video. Lesson script-following is optional direction. |
| BR-57 | A new child profile/run has an age range of 36–120 months with versioned age bands 36–71, 72–107 and 108–120 months. A profile aged 121–155 months does not receive new runs; a run accepted before the age reaches 121 months may complete per the accepted eligibility snapshot and the authorization/safety/lifecycle guards; a legacy draft is read/delete only. |
| BR-58 | A lesson audience may be a Parent group or specific people; each audience membership/grant can be revoked. |
| BR-59 | A Parent sends feedback attached to a lesson delivery; Teacher/Manager/Admin reply within the school scope; no direct chat or media upload is opened. |
| BR-60 | Billing owns quota usage by school organization and Parent account; Teacher usage is counted against school quota, Parent usage against Parent quota. There is no Teacher-owned purchase/owner pool or cross-scope fallback; a School Manager may internally allocate/reclaim unused quota from the School balance; the unused Teacher allocation automatically returns to the School when the Teacher leaves the organization, and consumed usage still belongs to the School. Included is used first, top-up FIFO; included expires at the end of the month and top-up does not expire until used up, per BR-38/Rev71. The example units/prices in §31.7 are only a review assumption, not production config. AI Policy owns compute reservation separately. Purchase/payment authority, School allocation permission, top-up no-expiry and unused-allocation return are chosen in §31.20/Rev71; renewal failure, handling after `VIDEO_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`, auto-renew timing against calendar-month grants and provider settlement remain open. |


### 31.8.2 Approved plan supersession — behavior matrix

Invariant in every row: an accepted occurrence/run/job retains the exact approved plan revision and LessonVersionRefV1 version/hash it accepted; it is never silently retargeted to latest. Completion must pass the existing expected-revision/input/context/lesson hash fence before writing to its owning aggregate. Owner-selected behavior is: accepted-not-queued and queued work is cancelled; running work is cancellation-requested and fenced, with late results purged; READY/playable output remains pinned to the immutable old version. No automatic accepted-quota refund or extra sample claim is created. Exact commands, receipts and cleanup timing remain OPEN-TECHNICAL.

| Supersession arrives while old pinned work is… | Technical invariant | StoryRun / AI Job | Sketch Deck / approval / occurrence | Sample claim / quota / projection | Owner choice still required |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Accepted, not yet queued | Preserve accepted plan/hash and admission receipts; idempotent replay resolves to same accepted intent. | Cancel before queueing; do not retarget or create replacement work automatically. | Old deck remains associated with its old hash; approval does not transfer. Exact cleanup event remains technical. | Keep accepted quota receipt; do not create another sample claim. | Cancellation receipt and cleanup timing remain technical. |
| Queued | Fence dequeue/worker start by expected revision; a late queue message must not substitute the new plan. | Cancel queued work; retries/replay cannot restart it against the new plan. | Old output remains linked to old hash; do not transfer approval/deck to new plan. | Keep accepted quota receipt; no automatic refund or extra claim. | Exact cancel command, receipt and replacement-run policy remain technical/product follow-up. |
| Running | Completion compares exact pinned hashes and expected revision; duplicate callback/replay is idempotent. | Request cancellation and fence the old revision; purge late results after reconciliation. | Late result cannot replace current content or transfer approval. | Keep accepted quota receipt; do not create an extra sample claim. Projection must not overwrite new revision. | Provider-outcome reconciliation and purge confirmation remain technical. |
| Ready but not yet played/projected | Preserve exact output provenance and old immutable plan/version pin. | Job terminal result remains on the original job; no rerun/retarget on read. | Keep old READY/playable output pinned to its old plan; new approval does not mutate it. | Do not create another successful sample claim or refund accepted quota. | Any separate removal request follows normal lifecycle; no implicit supersession hide. |
| Failed | Retain failure against the accepted old revision for audit/reconciliation. | Retry only under the existing bounded retry and idempotency policy; no implicit new-plan retry. | New plan may have independent work; old failure does not approve or invalidate it. | No inferred quota refund; pre-playable Teacher failure does not consume the successful sample slot under existing rule. | Whether a new command is allowed and its cost/claim behavior. |
| Cancelled | Keep cancellation receipt and old pin; reject late completion by expected revision/state fence. | Do not restart against the new plan on replay. | No silent approval/deck transfer. | Keep accepted quota; no extra claim. | Reuse/replacement occurrence command remains separate and cannot bypass sample/quota invariants. |

No transition in this table is a newly approved state. Exact stale/cancel events, owner command and audit fields must be closed before state diagrams or DDL are approved. Cross-owner billing and AI Policy receipts remain separate owner transactions joined by idempotent saga/reconciliation, never a shared SQL transaction.

### 31.9 Additional non-functional requirements and acceptance

| ID | Priority | Non-functional requirement |
|---|---|---|
| NFR-34 | M | Authorization deny-by-default, tenant isolation, and profile/lesson grant revocation must be applied uniformly across API, download, token, cache, and job dispatch; changes are audited metadata-only. |
| NFR-35 | M | Document filtering must be traceable: source hash → parser/filter version → allowlisted field → Teacher correction → AI draft; the raw original must not leak into a model request or telemetry. |
| NFR-36 | M | Cross-role profile grants and school feedback must be restricted by tenant/scope and audited. Child-media access by Manager/Platform Admin is deny by default in V1; any future exception requires a separate owner approval with permission, purpose/reason, expiry, and audit. |
| NFR-37 | M | Lesson version, generated visual, source file, and remix must retain lineage; a derived version must not overwrite the source. |
| NFR-38 | M | V1 content is in Vietnamese; target children are aged 3–10, Montessori-inspired, child-centered, with the adult as guide/observer/supporter. |

| AC | Given | When | Then |
|---|---|---|---|
| AC-46 | There are a Parent, Teacher, Manager, and Admin in two organizations | An actor reads lesson/profile/feedback/quota | Only permissions granted within the tenant/resource scope succeed; every revoke is audited. |
| AC-47 | A Teacher creates a reusable lesson and two occurrences | The lesson is updated to a new version | Old occurrences keep the pinned version; the lesson source is not modified backwards. |
| AC-48 | A Teacher lesson is in SCHOOL state, or a Platform Admin authors a public lesson | The author requests PUBLIC | A Teacher lesson is published only after School Manager approval; a Platform Admin-authored lesson needs an independent Platform Admin reviewer with Content Admin permission; hide/unpublish revokes access, and history metadata is retained. |
| AC-49 | A Parent grants consent and invites a named Teacher to share a child profile | The Teacher accepts the invitation, then the Parent revokes | The grant is ACTIVE only after acceptance, with a 90-day term; each Parent profile may have multiple grants, each grant links one Managed Profile, and each Managed Profile has at most one active Parent link. Revoke immediately blocks new API/token/job; the two account/profile owners remain separate. |
| AC-50 | A Teacher uploads a lesson plan following the template | The parser/filter completes | AI receives only allowlisted structured fields that have passed Teacher preview/correction; the raw file/full text must not be sent. |
| AC-51 | AI creates a lesson or activity suggestion | Generation succeeds | The state is draft; it is not made public before an authorized person reviews/publishes it. |
| AC-52 | A lesson contains multiple outdoor activities, or a Parent runs without pinning a lesson | The Parent selects the main activity before the story and views the result after the video | The main activity is confirmed/pinned to the run before the script and is the story context. After the video, no-pin returns the main activity from the run snapshot; lesson pin returns all activities of the exact version and marks the main activity. No new catalog query after the video. |
| AC-53 | Parent/Teacher does not enable line-sketch | A drawing session starts | No sketch AI job, asset, or quota usage is created. |
| AC-54 | A Parent preset has a script direction, or a Teacher plan has an approved objective/activity/safety | An adult confirms the script revision and requests a video | The Parent direction/Teacher approved plan is kept within the content limit; the new video follows the drawing/profile and does not reuse another video. Default/override limit remains OPEN. |
| AC-55 | The VLM subject confidence/mapping does not fit the drawing | The adult corrects the subject | The system requests confirmation, creates a new revision, and only then suggests again. |
| AC-56 | A Teacher occurrence has a set of children eligible under the current consent/assignment policy | The system requests a sample video | One child is selected with equal probability; the Teacher confirms before run/job/quota/claim; a stale candidate is reselected at most once; an empty set stops with no fallback, creates no run/job/quota/claim, and does not reveal the reason for excluding each child; an occurrence has at most one successful sample. Evidence/concurrency/error schema remains OPEN. |
| AC-57 | A Parent profile has a grant with a Teacher of school A | A principal of organization B reads the profile/feedback | Denied, without revealing that the resource exists or its content. |
| AC-58 | A Teacher/Manager/Admin replies to a Parent's feedback | A Parent belonging to school A opens the feedback | Only people with permission in school A see the thread; no chat or media attachment is opened. |
| AC-59 | A Platform Admin/Teacher uses a PUBLIC lesson and requests AI to propose edits | Generation completes; an authorized person reviews the output | A draft/personal copy with lineage is created; the source version and old occurrences do not change. A Parent does not initiate suggestions; may edit only drafts that are granted permission. |
| AC-60 | A lesson that has been used is then unpublished | A user opens the library/history | Version, occurrence, and history metadata are retained; the right to search/download/open lesson content is revoked. Lesson content is reopened only after it is made public again or a new grant is given. |
| AC-61 | A Parent and a Teacher request generation from a valid scope | A video job is accepted after quota admission | Billing records Parent usage to the Parent account; Teacher sample usage to the quota of the school that owns the occurrence. Included is used first, top-up FIFO; the units/prices provisionally for review in §31.7 are not production prices. AI Policy has a separate compute reservation. |
| AC-62 | A child's age is at the boundary of 36, 71, 72, 107, 108, or 120 months | Profile/run validation | 36–71, 72–107, and 108–120 months belong respectively to three versioned age bands; outside 36–120 months, no new accept/run is allowed per §31.14. |
| AC-63 | Search of PUBLIC lessons across the platform | A user does not belong to the author organization | The public version can be found/used; SCHOOL/PRIVATE/SELECTED are not exposed. |
| AC-64 | A Manager/Platform Admin (including Content Admin permission) sends a request to view child media in V1 | A request exists, even though the principal has a platform role/permission | Deny by default, no media or capability returned. A role/permission does not by itself create an exception; a future exception workflow is tested only after a separate owner decision. |
| AC-65 | A Parent/Teacher uses a personalized profile and lesson | AI builds the story/video | Complies with the approved age band/context, Vietnamese content; the video is created specifically from the child's drawing. |
| AC-66 | A profile enters the 121–155 month range while a StoryRun was accepted before the age reached 121; the user opens a legacy draft or tries an edit/archive action | Attempt a new run; continue an accepted run; read/delete/edit/archive a draft; detection/open notice | The old run may complete per the eligibility snapshot at accept time and the current authorization/safety/lifecycle guards; a new run is rejected. A draft can be read/deleted; edit/archive is rejected. One notice is sent on detection and one notice on opening the draft, not repeated for the same profile/condition. |

Details without a source remain OPEN/BLOCKED; no route, field, class, state, DDL, or runtime is asserted on its own. |

### 31.10 Open decisions

The list below keeps the 17 topics unchanged so that Q-ID traces are not broken. `RESOLVED` only means the owner has chosen the target policy; it does not prove that the API, schema, UI, or runtime has been implemented. Parts not answered specifically remain OPEN.

1. **Age bands — RESOLVED.** The three versioned bands are 36–71, 72–107, and 108–120 months; store age in months, do not store the exact date of birth, and keep the band version used. A profile of 121–155 months must not receive a new run; a run accepted before the age reaches 121 months may complete per the accepted eligibility snapshot and the current guards; a draft is read/delete only. The backfill/band-transition method for legacy profiles is a technical gate. Impact: FR-PL-25, AC-62/66, O-16.
2. **Quota units/prices — PARTIAL.** The owner keeps the examples of Parent 2 included runs/month + top-up of 5 runs at ₫249.000 and School 30 included runs/month + top-up of 50 runs at ₫1.990.000 for review only, not production price/config. The Platform Admin manages the offer and price version; actual production values are entered before sale, not hardcoded from the examples. Parent/School buyer, School Manager allocation/reclaim, MoMo/VNPAY, monthly manual/opt-in auto-renew, one-time top-up purchase, top-up not expiring until used up, unused Teacher allocation automatically returned to the School when the Teacher leaves the organization, and no-refund-after-settlement have been chosen at §31.20/Rev71. Included first/top-up FIFO; included expires at month end. Renewal failure/exhaustion response, auto-renew timing and provider settlement remain OPEN. Impact: FR-PL-24, AC-61, Q193.
3. **Parent lesson history — RESOLVED.** The window is 30 days from the first delivery/open. Revoke/unpublish keeps only neutral metadata and does not reopen content. Mapping history/retention into the schema is still a technical gate. Impact: FR-PL-21/22, Q183.
4. **Teacher random-child — PARTIAL.** Select with equal probability within the eligible set; the Teacher must confirm; a stale candidate is reselected at most once; if the set is empty, stop; before confirmation, no run is created/accepted and no job, quota usage, or claim is created. No fallback and no disclosure of the reason for excluding each child. Detailed eligibility/consent predicate, selection evidence, concurrency/idempotency, and error contract remain OPEN. Impact: UC-05, FR-T-10/FR-PL-20, AC-56.
5. **Script-following — PARTIAL.** The Parent keeps the preset's script direction; the Teacher keeps the approved objective/activity/safety; the adult confirms the final revision before the video. Default on/off, override scope, and the revision-pin contract remain OPEN. Impact: FR-PL-17/18, AC-54/55.
6. **School quota authority/allocation — PARTIAL.** Parent and School are two separate quota owners. The School Manager is assigned to buy quota for the organization, grant a hard allocation to Teachers belonging to the school from the available school balance, and reclaim the unused portion; when a Teacher leaves the organization, the unused allocation is automatically returned to the School balance, while consumed usage still belongs to the School. A Teacher has no right to buy or own a separate pool. Teacher usage is deducted from the quota of the organization that owns the occurrence; no cross-fallback. Included first, then top-up FIFO; included expires at month end and top-up does not expire until used up per BR-38/Rev71. Purchase/allocation authority and these two policy outcomes have been chosen; provider callback contract, exhausted response, auto-renew timing and settlement remain OPEN. Impact: FR-PL-24, AC-61.
7. **PUBLIC lesson created by Platform Admin — RESOLVED.** Teacher-authored PUBLIC requires School Manager approval; Platform Admin-authored PUBLIC requires independent review by another Platform Admin with Content Admin permission. Review/publish event schema and moderation evidence are technical gates. Impact: FR-PL-05, AC-48/60.
8. **Profile sharing — PARTIAL.** A Parent profile has 0..n grants; each grant points to one opaque Managed Profile; each Managed Profile has at most one active Parent link; the named Teacher must accept; 90-day term; Parent revoke is immediate; Delivery Recipient is an occurrence snapshot; no name matching or cross-owner SQL FK. Exact field allowlist, consent artifact, physical owner/schema, renewal and school/class invite remain OPEN. Impact: FR-PL-07/08, AC-46/49/57/64.
9. **AI visual/slide — PARTIAL.** The output is a draft, has provenance, and needs a reviewer; decks are not exported as PPTX/PDF. Asset owner, review criteria, reviewer authority for assets from a public lesson, and publish conditions remain OPEN. Impact: FR-PL-12/14, AC-51.
10. **Q192 / answer 46 source — OPEN-OWNER.** The owner does not remember the original question. "Yes, allow" does not grant permission or expand scope; there is no new requirement until the question and the subject/purpose are recovered.
11. **Child-media exception — V1 RESOLVED; future flow OPEN.** Platform Admin/Content Admin/School Manager must not view child media in V1. A future exception, if needed, must be a separate process with owner approval; the absence of a process does not block V1 deny-by-default. Impact: NFR-36, AC-64.
12. **Parent personal copy — PARTIAL.** The copy is reusable, PRIVATE, keeps source-version lineage, and does not modify the published source; a delete request has a 30-day recovery window. Exact editable fields, data owner/schema and retention after recovery remain OPEN. Impact: FR-PL-03/17.
13. **Parent does not use Lesson Preset — RESOLVED; Rev66 clarification.** Parent selects one main activity before the story on a catalog-first basis; no-pin returns the run-pinned activity after the video, while lesson pin returns all activities at their exact version and marks the main selection. API, snapshot and recovery details remain OPEN-TECHNICAL.
14. **Role and permission — PARTIAL.** Server-side authorization by role/scope/permission version and deny-by-default; Content Admin is a permission of Platform Admin; child media is denied in V1. Principal/assignment persistence, resolver/cache/revoke implementation, force-close mapping and audit field schema remain OPEN-TECHNICAL. Impact: FR-PL-01/08, NFR-34/36.
15. **Approved-plan supersession — RESOLVED at policy level.** Accepted-not-queued/queued work is cancelled; running work is cancellation-requested and fenced, with late results purged; READY/playable output retains its immutable old pin. No automatic quota refund or additional sample claim. Commands/receipts, reconciliation and purge evidence remain OPEN-TECHNICAL. Impact: §31.8.2, FR-T-08/20.
16. **Legacy profile 121–155 months — PARTIAL.** No new accept/run; a run accepted before the profile reaches 121 months may complete using its accepted eligibility snapshot, subject to current authorization, safety and lifecycle controls. An existing draft remains read/delete only, with one notice on detection and one when opened; edit/archive is not approved. Migration/backfill mechanics remain OPEN. Do not clamp or delete source data by inference. Impact: FR-PL-25, AC-66, profile/run admission and migration gates.
17. **Audit metadata/retention — PARTIAL.** Audit is metadata-only and excludes raw child media/content. Retention targets are 180 or 365 days by record type; exact type-to-duration map, field allowlist, audit authorization and physical mapping remain OPEN-TECHNICAL. Impact: NFR-34/36, data dictionary/physical ERD gate.

18. **SVG source-class behavior — OPEN-TECHNICAL.** Map `STROKE_READY`, `REQUIRES_MANUAL_AUTHORING`, `STATIC_ONLY` and `REJECTED` to versioned API/UI outcomes. Candidate safe rule: Parent has no manual authoring tool; rejected SVG is never rendered; unavailable-guide states never block mandatory capture. Classification names and exact fallback are not frozen.
19. **Guide/presentation quality review — OPEN-TECHNICAL.** Reviewer count, score aggregation, disagreement handling and adjudication are undecided. Rev83.3's proposed 4/5 recognizability/teachability floor and 2-point disagreement threshold are suggestions only, not approved requirements.
20. **Authoring/revision semantics — OPEN-TECHNICAL.** Decide supported guide edits, local undo/redo history, server draft revisions, published revision immutability, autosave concurrency/conflict handling and idempotency before API freeze.
21. **Renderer performance — OPEN-TECHNICAL.** Define browser/renderer versions, device classes, path/asset limits, sample size, cold/warm measurements and SLO. Emulator functional behavior and low-end physical Android performance are separate evidence categories; no numeric target is approved.
22. **API and persistence alignment — OPEN-TECHNICAL.** Rev83.3 API V2 routes remain proposals, not mounted contracts. Reconcile version, request/response/job/error lifecycle, guide/presentation revision and asset ownership, DTR, DDL and migration before implementation; do not infer entities/routes.
23. **Failure recovery — OPEN-TECHNICAL.** Distinguish capability disabled, catalog miss, worker unavailable, timeout and quality rejection. Parent capture must remain available and Teacher can continue lesson editing. Exact status codes, retries and recovery UI remain open.
### 31.10.1 Tracking the ten readiness groups for diagrams

This table tracks cross-cutting dependencies; the status is the level of input currently available, not SRS/diagram sign-off. No individual owner has been designated in the records.

| Group | Existing trace | Owner/status per current records | Remaining work to close |
|---|---|---|---|
| 01 Teacher Random Child | UC-05, FR-T-10, FR-PL-20, AC-56; Q-01; D-06 | Owner policy selected; PARTIAL | Equal probability, Teacher confirmation, max one stale-candidate reselect, empty-set stop/no fallback and no pre-confirmation run/job/quota/claim are set. Exact consent/eligibility predicates, evidence, concurrency and API/UI contracts remain open. |
| 02 Profile Sharing & Identity Matching | M-11/M-15; D-09; Q-04; §§31.3/31.6 | Owner policy selected; PARTIAL | Cardinality, named Teacher acceptance, 90-day grant, immediate revoke and occurrence-level recipient snapshot are set. Field allowlist, physical owner/mapping, renewal and school/class invitation remain open. |
| 03 Role & Permission | M-03/M-04/M-05; D-07; Q-13; D-146 | Policy partly set; PARTIAL / OPEN-TECHNICAL | Content Admin is a Platform Admin permission; child media is denied in V1; target auth is server-side by role/scope/version and deny-by-default. Persistence/resolver/cache/revoke and force-close evidence remain open. |

### 31.10.2 Seven diagrams still lacking inputs — readiness, updated per Rev67

The owner-supplied checklist reviewed for the earlier amendment identifies its source SRS as Rev61; it is review input only. A follow-up assessment supplied on 2026-11-13 labels itself Rev65. Both are review inputs, not product authority or proof of diagram completion. The current product authority is the latest revision of the canonical FEAT-063 v2.7-draft working SRS (see the revision table); at the time of this assessment it was Rev67, based on the owner-selected Rev57 source and local amendments through Rev67; it remains unsigned. The checklist says Context, Use Case, Workflow and System Architecture have sufficient inputs for logical TO-BE completion; that statement is not independently revalidated here and does not sign off those four views. The Rev65 follow-up is reconciled against Rev66/Rev67 at §31.18. No diagram artifact is created or signed off here.

`PARTIAL` means one or more required inputs are not yet traceable to an approved requirement/companion/source. `BLOCKED` means a required same-baseline or physical source is missing. `DONE` for an input document, `READY-TO-DRAW`, and `SIGNED-OFF` are separate milestones. `READY-TO-DRAW` requires all nodes/edges/guards/fields/operations in scope to have an approved or explicitly target-labeled source, with no material TBD. `SIGNED-OFF` is a separate owner/design approval after drawing. A TO-BE view must never be labeled as implemented/as-built without source evidence.

The Rev57 source-only scan in §32.7 is not proof of the current source tree. | B5 package/dependency scan |

#### Ten-case cross-view coverage

The checklist scenario labels are review-input indexes, not new APIs, route IDs, class names, state names or approved contracts. The checklist contains ten cross-view cases. The matrix below spells out what each case must show in each view; a missing source detail stays open instead of being filled by diagram prose.

|---:|---|---|

| 3 | O-14 authorization/revoke and O-11 child-media denial | D04: authorize principal, role, scope and permission version before read/write; deny fail-closed. <br>D05: carry authorization revision and conflict/deny outcome; do not leak whether a denied resource exists. <br>D06: denied/expired/revoked UX; no V1 child-media access screen, including for Content Admin. <br>D07: resolver responsibility for principal/resource/action/scope/version; exact signature remains open. <br>D08: keep role/assignment/grant version with its owner and use typed refs; no inferred Firebase role or cross-owner FK. <br>D09: use sourced revoke/assignment transitions and reject invalid commands. <br>D11: Identity/BE1 and resource-owner ports; no direct owner-table mutation. |

If none fit, exactly three quality-gated drafts may be produced. <br>D05: trace catalog filter → Qwen3.5 eligible candidate IDs/order/reasons → backend membership validation/canonical hydration; exclude raw artwork/audio and identifiers. A replacement set excludes shown/rejected candidates. <br>D06: activity selection is required before story; suggestion errors leave a recoverable selection state and cannot silently bypass it. After playback, no-pin returns the run-pinned activity without a catalog query; lesson pin returns the full exact-version list and marks the selected activity. <br>D07: lesson/version pin is optional and immutable. <br>D08: persist activity snapshot and presentation/guide revisions only after same-baseline DTR review; do not infer tables. <br>D09: source states/transitions require an approved lifecycle inventory. <br>D11: route through application/lesson/catalog boundaries; mounted status needs source evidence. |

Temporary units/prices are not production configuration. |
| 7 | Safety validation and regeneration | D04: unsafe/failed/review branches, hide old output on accepted replacement, and keep rewrite/scene/video retry counters separate. <br>D05: worker result, safety callback, duplicate/late result and retry correlation; exact schemas remain B3-open. <br>D06: blocked/review/retry/regenerate states without replaying hidden output. <br>D07: validation, generation and asset-lineage responsibilities. <br>D08: map output status and provenance/lineage refs; do not infer physical columns. <br>D09: keep StoryRun, VideoGeneration and AI Job transitions separate; unsafe output cannot become playable. <br>D11: Story–AI Jobs–Core Asset boundaries through ports/events. |

Migration remains open. |

Across all applicable rows, O-17 audit stays metadata-only, excludes child names/media/content, and uses the selected 180/365-day retention target by record type; exact type-to-duration mapping, fields, authorization and physical mapping remain open. B1=Data/Physical; B2=State/Activity; B3=API/Event/Sequence; B4=Screen/Navigation; B5=Class/Package. The Data Table Register remains separately labeled v2.5-draft and is not aligned to SRS Rev66; this cross-document baseline mismatch blocks physical ERD completion. Logical/physical ERD cannot be marked complete from the supplied checklist alone.

The suggested P0–P7 work order in the review note is a planning aid, not a new product requirement or evidence that its companion artifacts exist. B1 data/physical inputs are prerequisite to finalizing persisted keys/receipts; B2/B3 state and contract detail must reconcile; B4 follows sourced navigation/API boundaries; B5 must align classes with data and contracts. Detailed execution, approval and artifact tracking remain in the feature plan/evidence records.

<a id="srs-31-10-3"></a>
### 31.10.3 Minimum records required to close each diagram input gate

This section defines the minimum trace and evidence needed to assess the seven views in §31.10.2. It does not claim that the catalogs, diagrams, runtime routes, database schema, or source scans already exist. `OPEN` means a decision/source is missing; `OPEN-TECHNICAL` means policy exists but its technical contract/evidence is missing; `NOT VERIFIED` means current-source proof was not collected; `BLOCKED` means a required baseline/source is unavailable. `TO-BE` and `PROPOSED` describe target design only. `SOURCE-PRESENT` and `MOUNTED-OBSERVED` require attributable source evidence; runtime wiring also needs an exact commit and observation date. A complete checklist or template is not evidence of `READY-TO-DRAW`. `SIGNED-OFF` requires a separate authorized review after drawing.

#### D04 — Activity

Maintain an `ACTIVITY_SCENARIO_CATALOG` for A01–A10. These are review aliases, not new use cases or approved diagram names.

| Alias | Scenario coverage |
|---|---|
| A01 | Parent with an exact pinned lesson version: topic/lesson/guide are optional before the mandatory drawing capture; validate current access and pin ref/version/hash; compare VLM subject with selected topic/lesson and allow at most one recapture; after adult subject/context confirmation, select one main activity from the exact pinned version before story; obtain separate compute/quota admissions; pass technical and safety gates before playback; reauthorize before returning activities from the exact pin and deny content/token if access was revoked. |
| A02 | Parent without a lesson pin: optional topic/guide and mandatory capture; mismatch may allow a topic change or one recapture; after adult subject/context confirmation, select one main activity from currently accessible PUBLIC/SHARED lesson activities before story. If none fit, AI may produce exactly three quality-gated drafts; replacements exclude shown/rejected choices. Suggestion failure/unknown cannot bypass selection or proceed to story; exact recovery is OPEN-TECHNICAL. After video return only the run-pinned choice, without a new catalog query or additional activity list. |
| A03 | Teacher occurrence/sample, approved plan, optional deck, eligible-child selection and Teacher confirmation; one child and one successful sample per occurrence. |
| A04 | Empty candidate set, stale candidate with at most one reselect, revoke/assignment change and duplicate/concurrent confirmation; no run/job/quota usage/claim before Teacher confirmation. |
| A05 | Named Teacher profile-share invitation, acceptance/rejection, expiry, renewal only if approved, revoke and denial of later reads or new unaccepted work. |
| A06 | Lesson upload/filter, Teacher preview/correction, review, reject/hide and publication under BR-51 reviewer rules. |
| A07 | Script confirmation and video admission, separate AI Policy and Billing receipts, dispatch, worker/safety outcomes and reconciliation. |
| A08 | Plan supersession at accepted-not-queued, queued, running and READY/playable points; cancel/fence late work and preserve the old immutable pin. |
| A09 | Adult idle reminder/closeout, cancel/delete, lock release and media cleanup under existing lifecycle rules. |
| A10 | School capacity, payment callback, grace/downgrade and read-only behavior only where current SRS confirms V1 scope; otherwise mark `N/A` with a sourced rationale. |

For every action/decision, record scenario/step ID; UC/BR/FR/AC/decision trace; trigger and actor/swimlane; input and typed references with revision/hash; preconditions; action/decision; both true and false guard outcomes; owner write or explicit `NO WRITE`; sourced domain-state change or `NO CHANGE`; event/receipt or `NONE`; next step; failure/deny/empty/stale/timeout/cancel/revoke path; timeout/retry/reconcile/compensation rule; source/evidence and status. Every decision needs both branches and a safe destination. Labels such as `SUCCESS`, `DENY`, `EMPTY`, `STALE`, `TIMEOUT`, `CANCEL`, `REVOKE`, `FAILED`, and `AWAITING_RECONCILIATION` are analysis outcomes, not domain states unless separately defined by a source.

Keep the 144-hour adult reminder, 168-hour adult closeout and six-hour accepted AI-job deadline separate (§§6.1.1, 6.2, BR-39). Keep the counters distinct: at most three story rewrites after the initial story; at most two technical retries per scene after its initial attempt; and at most two full-video regeneration requests after the initial video request (BR-11/13/17/45). Random-child reselect is at most once and only for a stale candidate (§5.2/UC-05/AC-56). Show fork/join only for actual parallel work, with its join condition and partial-failure path. Billing quota and AI Policy compute remain separate owner steps; do not dispatch until both admission receipts are confirmed or reconciled.

#### D05 — Sequence

Maintain a `SEQUENCE_MESSAGE_INDEX` for S01–S12. Include only participating lifelines; a proposed provider/service lifeline is not evidence that it exists or is mounted.

| Alias | Message coverage |
|---|---|
| S01 | Parent with exact lesson pin: capture is mandatory and topic/guide optional; validate current access and resolve/pin exact lesson ref/version/hash; perform subject mismatch handling with at most one recapture; after adult confirmation select one main activity from the pinned version before story; receive separate AI Policy compute and Billing quota receipts; run job/result/technical+safety gates; play video; reauthorize before returning activities from that exact pin, denying content/token if current access was revoked. |
| S02 | Parent without lesson pin: after mandatory capture, VLM and adult subject/context confirmation, required pre-story selection filters currently renderable activities from accessible published/shared lessons. Qwen3.5 receives only the minimized allowlist (age band, allowed interest tags, selected topic, adult-confirmed subject, visible features and confirmed narration when present) and returns eligible IDs/reasons; backend validates membership and hydrates canonical content. No image/audio bytes or identity. If none fit, it may draft exactly three temporary candidates through quality gate; replacement requests exclude shown/rejected IDs. Story waits for adult selection. Suggestion failure/unknown cannot silently bypass the required choice; exact recovery remains OPEN-TECHNICAL. After video return the run-pinned main activity only, with no catalog query or additional list. |
| S03 | Teacher eligibility response, confirmation, stale/empty/reselect handling and one-successful-sample claim. |
| S04 | Named profile-share invitation/accept/revoke, grant revision and effects on later reads or unaccepted jobs. |
| S05 | Lesson upload, extraction/filter, author preview/correction, reviewer decision and publish/hide. |
| S06 | Story intent, separate AI Policy compute reservation and Billing quota receipt, denial/ambiguity reconciliation, then AI Jobs dispatch only after accepted receipts. |
| S07 | Queue/worker/provider result, validation, safety, asset commit, duplicate/late/out-of-order callback and cleanup. |
| S08 | Adult rejection, hide old playback, script reconfirmation and bounded full-video regeneration within the same run. |
| S09 | Plan supersession cancel/fence, immutable old pin, stale callback rejection and reconciliation. |
| S10 | Assignment transfer, Story lock/compensation, authorization cache revocation and distinct explicit emergency force-close command. |
| S11 | Signed payment event verification, provider-event dedupe, payment receipt, entitlement/capacity change and grace handling. |
| S12 | Scheduler/inbox/push best-effort, idle closeout, cancel/delete, object purge, outbox and stuck-work reconciliation. |

For each message, record message ID; sender/receiver and owner; sync/async; command/query/event and version; request fields with types/requiredness only when sourced; response/error envelope and UI handling only when sourced; authorization/scope and expected revision; idempotency/correlation/causal key; owner transaction and receipt; timeout/retry/dedupe/out-of-order/dead-letter/reconciliation behavior; source commit/date and evidence status. For each event/callback also record envelope, source owner, consumer, delivery mode, causal/reference key and handling for duplicate, out-of-order, timeout and dead-letter cases. Mark each edge `TO-BE`, `PROPOSED`, `SOURCE-PRESENT`, `MOUNTED-OBSERVED`, or `NOT VERIFIED`. HTTP status, error code, endpoint, payload and event shape stay `OPEN` if no current source defines them. Cross-owner writes remain separate owner transactions; ambiguous admission receipts use the same idempotency key and do not permit dispatch while unresolved.

#### D06 — Screen Flow

Maintain a `SCREEN_CATALOG`, `SCREEN_NAVIGATION_MAP`, and UI error/state matrix. Cover the role surfaces listed in §21.5: Android Parent, Web Teacher, School Manager, Platform Admin content administration and the operator dashboard where applicable. These are surface families, not approved screen IDs or routes. Record a stable screen ID only if sourced/approved; otherwise mark ID and route `PROPOSED` or `OPEN`. For each screen capture app/surface, role/resource scope, design owner/evidence, entry trigger/preconditions, actual route/deep link or proposed status, authorized data/query/API and revision, visible-data allowlist, primary/secondary action and resulting command, and each guard-controlled outgoing edge. Include Back, Cancel, Resume, Refresh, deep link, background/foreground, token expiry, offline/reload behavior and async progress where applicable. Record loading, ready, empty, denied, expired, stale/conflict, quota exhausted, pending/reconciling, failed, retry and success as presentation outcomes, not domain-state names. Every branch needs a reachable safe exit; client navigation alone is not business confirmation. Do not invent screens/routes or imply design approval.

#### D07 — Class

Maintain a `CLASS_CONTRACTS` inventory and separate `CLASS_RELATIONS` view. A candidate source/ORM class is not automatically an approved domain class. For each included class/interface/port record source status (`TO-BE`/`PROPOSED`/`SOURCE-PRESENT`), owning module/layer, responsibility, typed attributes and nullability only when sourced, operation signatures/errors only when contracted, ports, relation/multiplicity, aggregate/tenant owner, invariants, version/lineage refs, dependency direction and BR/FR/AC/decision/API/data trace. Keep cross-owner references typed and opaque where required; do not infer cross-owner foreign keys or duplicate owner state. Keep missing details `OPEN` with the decision/source owner needed.

#### D08 — ERD Logical and Physical

Keep the two ERDs separate. For Logical ERD record entity/aggregate and owner, logical identifier/reference type, sourced attributes and lifecycle meaning, cardinality/optionality, tenant/resource scope, version/lineage, constraints/invariants and trace to the aligned data register and class/contract views. Keep the SRS target counts of 92 active and 48 legacy mappings distinct from a verified live schema; keep legacy/read-only mappings separate. Cross-owner references are typed contracts, not implicit joins or presumed SQL foreign keys.

For Physical ERD, tie every table/column to a reviewed same-baseline DTR/DDL source and record physical type, nullability, default, PK/FK/unique/check/index definition, delete/update action, owner, migration/backfill/rollback crosswalk and database/source evidence. Do not derive DDL from the logical model, examples, old DTR versions or §32 environment snapshots. The current Data Table Register remains v2.5-draft and is not aligned to SRS Rev66; therefore D08 Physical remains `BLOCKED`. Do not mark columns, constraints or migration behavior complete.

#### D09 — State Machine

Maintain a `STATE_INVENTORY` and `STATE_TRANSITION_MATRIX` per aggregate. Start only from states in current SRS §6.1.1 or a separately approved contract. For each aggregate record state source and initial/terminal role. For each transition record aggregate, source state, command/event and version, actor/owner, authorization and expected-version guard, target state or explicit `NO CHANGE`, owner write/effect, receipt/outbox event, response/error, invalid-transition behavior, timeout/retry/cancel/revoke/supersession behavior, compensation/reconciliation and source/evidence. Demonstrate reachable states with a valid exit or sourced recovery path. Keep StoryRun, VideoGeneration, AiJob, billing/payment and lesson-review lifecycles separate. For aggregates without a sourced inventory in §6.1.1 or another approved contract (for example, ProfileShareGrant, LessonVersion publication/review, deletion/purge artifact lifecycle and quota-admission receipt), leave state names/transitions `OPEN` until a source supplies them. Do not apply LessonOccurrence states to a separate LessonVersion lifecycle or infer enums. A UI outcome or reconciliation label is not a domain state unless defined by a source. Adult timers and AI-job deadline remain independent; late/duplicate callbacks cannot bypass revision fences, authorization, safety or immutable pins.

#### D11 — Package

Maintain separate `TO-BE` package ownership/dependency and `AS-BUILT` source-dependency records. For target packages record path/name as `PROPOSED` if absent, owner/layer, public visibility, import policy, allowed direction and contract. For as-built evidence record exact repository commit, scan date/tool/scope, source/destination package paths, dependency kind (`import`, `port`, `event`, HTTP, adapter/DI), source evidence, purpose, allowed/forbidden result and any cycle; document cycle disposition/resolution and its reviewer/approval where required. Verify composition entrypoints, dependency-injection registrations, mounted routers, queue/worker composition, event consumers and task registrations against that exact revision. Report pass/fail only for checks actually run. §32.7 records a historical static import cycle between `infrastructure.canvas_render` and `infrastructure.canvas_render.renderer` at commit `ae5707ca188d01d9719ed959b62d768ae927912d`; use this only as a lead for a fresh exact-commit scan, not as a current cycle result or proof of closure. §32 is an environment-specific snapshot, not a current exact-commit scan. Do not claim a proposed package/router is present or mounted without source evidence.

#### Cross-view closeout

For AC-67, map each of the ten policy cases in §31.10.2 to applicable D04/D05/D06/D07/D08 Logical/D08 Physical/D09/D11 record IDs, or give a reasoned `N/A`. Record mismatches, resolution/source, approver when a product decision is required, and status. O-17 audit remains metadata-only; exact event-to-retention mapping, field allowlist, access authority and physical schema remain open. A complete catalog or blank template is not evidence of `READY-TO-DRAW`; readiness does not imply `SIGNED-OFF` or production implementation.

## Rev31 backend implementation crosswalk (additive; pending SRS content review)

The v2.3 product-design baseline and requirements remain an unsigned draft. Rev31 implementation source adds three Lesson-owned support tables: `lesson.library_source_documents`, `lesson.library_document_revisions`, and `lesson.command_receipts`. They keep reusable-library uploads separate from occurrence-owned source documents and provide durable command idempotency. The current source-derived implementation crosswalk is 38 retained/refactored + 54 new = 92 active, plus 48 legacy/read-only = 140 projected physical mappings. This does not change product requirements, sign off the SRS/Data Table Register, or claim that the migration has run. See Data Table Register section 14.4 and EV-063-24.

### 31.12 Owner clarifications after Q147–Q195 (2026-11-06)

- **Q159 role resolution:** Platform Admin and Teacher may ask AI to draft lesson/activity suggestions. Parent may edit an authorized lesson draft only; Parent cannot start topic-based lesson-plan suggestion generation or publish a source version. Rev62's Parent activity preview is a distinct StoryRun-scoped suggestion from a captured/confirmed drawing subject, is not a lesson draft/catalog entry, does not publish content and does not change that role rule.
- **AI development direction:** Qwen3.5-VL is the current owner-selected model family for drawing understanding and structured story/storyboard development; SVG catalog + deterministic vector engine is the Rev66 target for line-sketch and lesson visuals, with structured Qwen drafts only when enabled and needed; FLUX.3 is superseded and not a fallback, apart from the narrow optional Teacher Sketch Deck picture of §31.21. Whisper is the speech-to-text direction; SAM 2.1 is optional for image segmentation. Modal is the inference-hosting direction. Exact checkpoints, runtime revisions, license/safety review, measured quality and costs are not approved by this documentation note.
- These clarifications do not remove Parent/Teacher workflows, relax the filtered-lesson input gate, or claim that Modal is wired end-to-end into the main API. The current build snapshot is §32.

### 31.13 Owner clarifications from Rev52 review (2026-11-06)

- "Admin nội dung" is a permission of Platform Admin, not an independent product role. The review/publish/moderate permission does not grant the right to view child profiles/media.
- Platform Admin access to child media is denied by default and awaits a separate process approved by the owner. Until then, every request is denied; approver, scope/purpose, expiry, audit detail and break-glass remain open.
- Teacher video generation consumes only the quota of the school organization that owns the occurrence; a Teacher has no right to buy or own a separate quota pool; an assigned School Manager may allocate/reclaim the unused quota portion internally; the unused portion is automatically returned to the School when the Teacher leaves the organization, while usage already consumed remains with the School. A Parent account keeps a separate quota. Top-up does not expire until used up. Rev61 units/prices remain review examples, not production config; specific prices are managed by Admin through offer/version. Renewal failure, exhaustion and other role changes remain open.
- The current SRS/Data Table Register target count is 92 active + 48 legacy = 140 projected mappings; 89 + 48 = 137 is the historical v2.3 baseline. Neither figure replaces production inventory/DDL review.
